Do not cover it with heavy blankets or plastic, which trap moisture and prevent drying. • Elevation to Prevent Edema: Elevate the casted extremity on pillows above the level of the heart for the first 24 to 48 hours to promote venous return and prevent severe edema. • Neurovascular Assessment (The 6 Ps): Inspect the exposed digits (toes or fingers) frequently for: Pain, Pallor (pale or cyanotic skin), Poikilothermia (coldness), Paresthesia (numbness or tingling), Paralysis (inability to wiggle toes/fingers), and Pulselessness (absent capillary refill >2 seconds). Immediately report any abnormal findings to the nurse, as severe swelling under an unyielding cast can cause Compartment Syndrome—an ischemic emergency that leads to permanent muscle necrosis and amputation within hours if not surgically released via fasciotomy. • Skin Protection: Keep the cast completely dry during sponge bathing. Never allow the client to insert coat hangers, knitting needles, or rulers down the cast to scratch an itch; inserting objects breaks skin integrity and introduces infection.
临床常用熟石膏绷带或新型玻璃纤维高分子材料浇筑固化形成石膏管型。传统石膏完全干燥需24至48小时（干燥过程中产热），而玻璃纤维石膏在15至30分钟内即可固化。支持工作者照护石膏患者的刚性规程： • 手掌托扶黄金准则（严禁用指尖）：在石膏尚未完全干燥固化前，搬动或翻转患肢时必须且只能用平平张开的双手「手掌」平稳托扶，严禁用指尖捏掐！指尖施力会在未干的石膏内壁压出永久性凹陷压痕，石膏干燥后该凹坑会持续压迫脆弱的皮下组织和微血管，极易在数天内引发坏死性深部压疮。 • 暴露空气自然干燥：未干石膏必须完全裸露于空气中自然风干。严禁用厚重棉被包裹或覆以塑料布，否则会阻碍水分蒸发并延长干燥周期。 • 抬高患肢减轻水肿：在石膏固定后的最初24至48小时内，用软枕将患肢垫高，使其高于心脏水平位置，促进静脉回流，预防肢端进行性重度水肿。 • 神经血管循环监测（6P征）：每班必须严密检查暴露在石膏外的指端或趾端：疼痛（Pain）、苍白或发绀（Pallor）、皮温冰凉（Poikilothermia）、麻木刺痛蚁走感（Paresthesia）、无法自主活动脚趾手指（Paralysis）以及毛细血管充盈时间延长超过2秒或动脉搏动消失（Pulselessness）。一旦发现指端青紫、剧痛或麻木，必须秒级向主管护士报告！因为石膏缺乏弹性伸缩，肢体水肿可引发灾难性的「骨筋膜室综合征」（Compartment Syndrome），若未在数小时内行外科筋膜切开减压，将导致前臂或小腿肌肉全层缺血坏死、残疾甚至被迫截肢。 • 严防潮湿与异物伸入：床上擦浴时必须使用双层塑料膜包裹石膏边缘防止受潮软化变形。严禁长者使用衣架、毛衣针或尺子探入石膏内搔抓止痒；伸入异物会划破皮肤造成隐匿性创口继发严重溃烂感染。
PAGE 776 · 第 776 页 Skeletal Traction Management & Osteomyelitis / 骨骼牵引固定装置护理与骨髓炎临床防治

TRACTION: PRINCIPLES AND SAFETY RULES

牵引治疗（Traction）：生物力学原理与安全管理红线
Traction applies a continuous pulling force in two directions to immobilize and align broken bone fragments, reduce painful muscle spasms, and correct deformities until internal fixation or solid union can occur. Traction is either Skin Traction (force applied to the skin and soft tissues via straps, foam boots, or adhesive wraps, such as Buck's traction) or Skeletal Traction (force applied directly to the bone via surgical pins, wires, or tongs surgically inserted through the bone, such as a Steinmann pin or Kirschner wire through the distal femur or proximal tibia). Absolute Traction Safety Rules for Support Workers: 1. Free-Hanging Weights: Traction weights must hang freely in the air at all times. NEVER lift, remove, support, bump, or set the weights on the floor or furniture; removing the pulling force causes severe muscle spasms and re-displacement of bone ends. 2. Ropes & Pulleys: Ensure ropes are centered squarely in the pulley grooves and not frayed, tangled, or touching the bed frame. 3. Body Alignment: Keep the client in straight body alignment with the pull of the traction. The client must not slide down toward the foot of the bed, which allows weights to rest on the floor. 4. Pin Site Infection Surveillance: For skeletal traction, inspect the entry and exit sites of the surgical pin daily for signs of infection: redness, swelling, warmth, pain, or purulent drainage.
牵引疗法（Traction）利用生物力学滑轮配重系统，沿骨折肢体长轴施加持续对抗的拉力与反牵引力，以维持骨折端对位对线、克服肌肉痉挛性回缩引起的短缩畸形，并在手术内固定前提供稳定固定。牵引主要分为皮牵引（通过海绵套靴、胶布直接粘贴皮肤牵拉软组织，如皮牵引Buck's traction，牵引力通常不超过3-5公斤）与骨牵引（骨科医生在无菌手术室将斯氏骨圆针Steinmann pin或克氏针Kirschner wire直接贯穿股骨髁上或胫骨结节骨质，牵引力可达10-15公斤）。 支持工作者必须坚守的牵引护理刚性安全红线： 1. 牵引砝码必须悬空（Free-Hanging）：悬挂于床尾的牵引重锤/砝码在全天候内必须保持「悬空悬垂状态」！严禁支持工作者徒手托举、挪动、卸除重锤，严禁使砝码触碰地板、床架或椅面。一旦拉力骤失，强大的骨骼肌痉挛会瞬间导致骨折端剧烈错位撕裂并引发患者难以忍受的剧痛。 2. 牵引绳与滑轮轴线：确保牵引绳索紧密嵌在滑轮凹槽中心顺畅滑动；绳索绝不可磨损起毛、缠绕打结，严禁与床档被褥发生摩擦阻滞。 3. 保持躯干轴线与对抗牵引：协助患者维持平卧位躯干躯体轴线对齐。防范患者身体向床尾下滑——一旦患者滑向床下，牵引重锤着地便完全丧失了牵引力。 4. 骨牵引针孔无菌观察：骨牵引钢针贯穿皮肉骨髓，是深部细菌入侵的直接通道。每日必须配合护士密切观察穿皮针孔处有无充血发红、肿胀热痛或渗出脓性分泌物，发现异常立即上报。

FIGURE 33.7: Skeletal traction for a femur fracture utilizing a Steinmann surgical pin inserted through the bone to maintain limb alignment and promote healing.

图 33.7：股骨骨折骨骼牵引装置。斯氏骨圆针（Steinmann pin）经股骨髁上骨质贯穿，利用滑轮与悬空悬垂砝码维持患肢纵向对准，减轻骨痉挛并促进骨折稳定愈合。

Osteomyelitis: Deep Bone Infection

骨髓炎：深部骨组织化脓性感染
Osteomyelitis is a severe inflammation or infection of the bone and bone marrow, most commonly caused by Staphylococcus aureus bacteria. Pathogens enter the bone through open compound fractures, penetrating surgical pin sites in skeletal traction, open reduction orthopedic surgeries, or by traveling through the bloodstream from a distant infection (hematogenous osteomyelitis). Symptoms include sudden high fever, chills, severe throbbing bone pain over the infected site, localized warmth, edema, and redness. Treatment requires weeks to months of intensive intravenous antibiotic therapy, surgical debridement of necrotic bone (sequestrectomy), and strict aseptic wound care.
骨髓炎（Osteomyelitis）是指由化脓性致病菌（最常见为金黄色葡萄球菌）引起的骨皮质、骨松质及骨髓组织的深部化脓性坏死性炎症。致病菌可通过开放性骨折创口直接侵入、经骨牵引穿皮针道深部逆行感染、人工关节置换手术污染，或由身体其他原发感染灶经血液循环播散至干骺端（血源性骨髓炎）。急性发作时患者呈现突发寒战高热、患肢局部剧烈跳痛触痛、皮温显著升高、局部红肿及关节活动受限。治疗需要持续数周至数月的超广谱静脉抗生素冲击治疗、手术彻底切除坏死死骨（死骨切除术），并施行严密无菌换药引流。
PAGE 777 · 第 777 页 Hip Fractures, Internal Fixation & Post-Operative Abduction / 髋部骨折病理、内固定手术与术后外展体位固定

HIP FRACTURES IN OLDER ADULTS

高龄长者髋关节骨折流行病学与术后康复
Fractured hips are among the most serious and common orthopedic injuries in older adults, especially older women, due to the high prevalence of osteoporosis. A fractured hip usually involves the upper third of the femur (either a femoral neck fracture or an intertrochanteric fracture). Falls are the overwhelming cause. Hip fractures carry high morbidity and mortality, frequently leading to prolonged immobility, pneumonia, deep vein thrombosis (DVT), and loss of independent ambulation. Surgical Management (Hip Pinning vs Arthroplasty): Treatment almost always involves urgent surgery to achieve early mobilization. Depending on the location and severity, surgeons perform internal fixation ('hip pinning' using cannulated screws, plates, or intramedullary compression hip screws) or partial/total hip replacement (hemiarthroplasty or total hip arthroplasty / THA, replacing the femoral head and acetabulum with metal/ceramic prostheses).
髋关节骨折（Fractured Hips）是高龄老年人——特别是绝经后骨质疏松女性最常见且最为致命的骨科创伤之一，常被称为高龄老人的「人生最后一次骨折」。骨折绝大多数发生于股骨近端上三分之一（股骨颈骨折或股骨转子间骨折），跌倒是导致骨折的最核心诱因。髋部骨折并发症极为凶险，长期卧床极易继发肺部坠积性肺炎、下肢深静脉血栓脱落导致肺栓塞、严重压疮及泌尿系感染，死亡率极高。 外科手术干预（内固定打钉 vs 关节置换）：现代老年医学极力主张急诊或限期手术干预以实现尽早离床活动。根据骨折解剖部位及移位程度，骨科常行内固定术（俗称「髋部打钉」Hip pinning——使用空心加压螺钉、滑动髋螺钉DHS或髓内钉固定），或施行人工股骨头置换（半髋置换）/ 全髋关节置换术（THA——用钛合金人工股骨头与超高分子聚乙烯髋臼杯完全替换磨损破坏的关节）。

Post-Operative Positioning: Hip Abduction Protocol

术后防脱位体位规程：全时段下肢外展固定
Following hip surgery, the support worker must adhere strictly to orthopedic positioning precautions to prevent dislodging surgical hardware or dislocating the prosthetic joint: • Continuous Abduction: The operated leg must be kept in an abducted position (away from the midline) AT ALL TIMES. An abductor pillow (a wedge-shaped foam cushion) or firm pillows must be placed between the client’s knees and ankles whenever the client is resting in bed or being turned. Never allow the client's legs to cross the midline! • Prevent External Rotation: Use a trochanter roll (a rolled towel or blanket tucked firmly under the greater trochanter from the crest of the ilium to mid-thigh) to prevent the hip and leg from rolling outward. • Avoid Hip Flexion Greater than 90 Degrees: Do not allow the client to bend forward at the waist beyond a 90-degree angle; do not raise the head of the bed higher than 45 degrees; ensure the client uses an elevated toilet seat and avoids low, soft chairs.
髋关节手术后，支持工作者必须坚决贯彻骨科防脱位体位保护规程，防止内固定松动拔出或人工假体头脱出髋臼： • 全时段保持患肢外展（Continuous Abduction）：患侧下肢在任何时刻都必须处于「外展位」（向身体外侧展开，远离正中线）。长者在平卧、休养或翻身时，双膝及双踝之间必须始终夹放专用的硬质海绵「外展楔形枕」（Abductor pillow）或坚实软枕。绝对严禁双腿交叉内收过身体中线！ • 严防下肢外旋（Prevent External Rotation）：在患侧大腿外侧（从髂嵴至股骨大转子中段）塞紧卷成筒状的坚固毛巾卷（大转子卷 Trochanter roll），阻止下肢自然向外撇倒外旋。 • 髋关节屈曲严禁超过90度：严禁长者弯腰前倾超过90度拾物；床头抬高角度不得超过45度；如厕必须使用专用加高马桶垫圈；严禁坐于低矮、下陷松软的沙发椅上。

FIGURE 33.8: Maintaining hip abduction after surgery. A, The operated hip is kept abducted when turning the client onto their side. B, An abductor wedge pillow is strapped securely between the knees to maintain proper alignment and prevent dislocation.

图 33.8：髋部术后维持下肢外展位图解。A：协助长者侧卧翻身时，双腿之间垫枕维持患髋外展。B：使用专用外展楔形海绵枕固定于双腿双膝之间，维持解剖对齐，彻底杜绝下肢交叉内收诱发的人工关节脱位。

PAGE 778 · 第 778 页 Gangrene, Amputation Management & Stump Shaping / 坏疽病理机制、截肢术与残端弹力塑形包扎

GANGRENE: TISSUE NECROSIS AND SURGICAL INTERVENTION

坏疽（Gangrene）：缺血坏死病理与外科清创截肢
Gangrene is the death (necrosis) of body tissue caused by an acute or chronic lack of blood supply, frequently complicated by secondary bacterial infection. Major causes include severe peripheral vascular disease, diabetes mellitus, severe frostbite, deep crushing trauma, and arterial embolisms. Dry gangrene occurs in severe arterial obstruction where the affected extremity (typically toes or feet) becomes cold, shriveled, painless, and turns completely black. Wet gangrene occurs when dead tissue becomes infected with anaerobic gas-producing bacteria, causing swelling, foul-smelling liquefaction, and rapidly spreading toxic sepsis. Immediate surgical amputation of the necrotic limb is necessary to save the client's life.
坏疽（Gangrene）是指因局部组织供血动脉发生急剧完全中断或重度狭窄，导致局部大块组织发生缺血性凝固性坏死（Necrosis），并常伴发厌氧菌等继发性感染。最主要的临床诱因包括晚期下肢闭塞性外周动脉疾病、糖尿病重度坏疽足、深度重度冻伤、压榨性碾碎伤以及大动脉急性血栓栓塞。干性坏疽（Dry gangrene）见于动脉缓慢闭塞而静脉通畅时，病变肢端（多见于脚趾或足背）逐渐冰冷干瘪、感觉完全丧失并变为炭黑硬结；湿性坏疽（Wet gangrene）则因坏死组织继发产气厌氧菌感染，呈现水肿发亮、伴随刺鼻恶臭的脓性软化坏死，极易并发毒血症感染性休克。一旦保守治疗无效，必须紧急施行外科截肢术以挽救患者生命。

FIGURE 33.9: Gangrene. Severe tissue necrosis of the foot resulting from complete peripheral arterial occlusion in a diabetic client, necessitating surgical amputation.

图 33.9：下肢坏疽典型外观。糖尿病并发下肢严重外周动脉完全闭塞导致的足趾缺血性坏死变黑，必须接受外科急诊截肢手术以阻断败血症蔓延。

Amputation Levels, Stump Shaping and Bandaging

截肢解剖平面、残端弹力加压塑形与包扎技巧
Amputation involves the surgical removal of all or part of an extremity. Lower extremity amputations are far more common than upper extremity amputations, categorized by anatomical level: Below-Knee Amputation (BKA / transtibial), Above-Knee Amputation (AKA / transfemoral), Syme's amputation (ankle disarticulation), or toe/forefoot amputations. Stump Care and Compression Bandaging: • Residual Limb Shaping: Before an amputee can be fitted with a permanent artificial limb (prosthesis), the residual limb (stump) must heal completely, shrink, and be shaped into a smooth, conical or cylindrical contour without excess flabby tissue or edema. • Figure-of-Eight Elastic Bandaging: The nurse or trained support worker wraps the stump using an elastic compression bandage applied in a figure-of-eight pattern (or applies an elastic residual limb shrinker sock). The bandage must provide graduated compression—tighter at the distal tip of the stump and gradually looser toward the body—to push lymphatic and venous fluid upward, prevent edema, and mold the limb for the prosthesis socket. Rewrap the bandage multiple times daily to maintain uniform compression.
截肢术（Amputation）是指通过外科手术切除肢体的全部或某一部分。下肢截肢在临床上显著多于上肢截肢，按解剖平面主要分为：膝下截肢（BKA——经胫骨截肢）、膝上截肢（AKA——经股骨截肢）、赛姆截肢（踝关节离断）以及足趾或前足局部截肢。 残端护理与弹力加压塑形包扎： • 残端塑形生理学要求：在为截肢长者定制安装永久性人工假肢（Prosthesis）之前，截肢残端（Stump）必须完全愈合消除水肿，并通过机械加压逐渐萎缩紧实，塑形成为圆锥形或圆柱形的结实残端，防止多余脂肪赘肉堆积或软组织水肿。 • 「8」字形弹力绷带包扎规范：护士或经过专门培训的支持工作者使用专用弹力压迫绷带，按照规范的「8」字形交叉缠绕手法（或使用医用弹力残端塑形套）进行加压包扎。弹力包扎必须遵循「梯度递减减压原则」——残端远侧末端压力最紧、越向近心端越逐渐放松，从而驱动淋巴与静脉水肿液向近心端回流，消除水肿并使残端适应假肢接受腔的紧密贴合。每日需多次重新拆开检查并重新包扎，确保压力均匀恒定。
FIGURES 33.10 & 33.11: Amputation Care and Prosthetics
图 33.10 与 33.11：假肢安装与残端弹力加压包扎图解
FIGURE 33.10: Modern upper-extremity arm prosthesis designed to restore reaching, grasping, and functional independence.
图 33.10：现代上肢手臂肌电假肢，旨在协助截肢患者恢复抓握、支撑与日常自主生活能力。
FIGURE 33.11: Mid-thigh above-knee amputation (AKA) stump securely wrapped with an elastic bandage in a graduated figure-of-eight pattern to shrink and shape the limb for a prosthetic socket.
图 33.11：膝上截肢（股骨中段）残端使用弹力绷带进行规范的「8」字形梯度包扎，以消除水肿、紧缩肌肉并重塑残端形状，为安装人工假肢接受腔奠定解剖基础。
PAGE 779 · 第 779 页 Phantom Pain, Prosthetic Care & Osteoarthritis (OA) / 幻肢痛机制、假肢日常维护与退行性骨关节炎

PHANTOM LIMB SENSATION AND PHANTOM PAIN

幻肢感觉与幻肢痛（Phantom Limb Pain）应对
Following an amputation, almost all clients experience phantom limb sensation—the uncanny, vivid feeling that the amputated body part is still present, attached, and moving. Many clients experience phantom limb pain: feeling severe burning, crushing, throbbing, twisting, or aching sensations originating from the amputated toes, foot, or hand. Phantom pain is not psychological imagination; it is real physical pain produced by the brain and severed peripheral nerve endings (neuromas) sending erratic signals across damaged neural pathways. The support worker must validate the client's pain, never dismiss it as 'imaginary,' and administer prescribed neuropathic pain medications or therapies (such as mirror therapy, massage, or TENS).
截肢手术后，绝大多数患者都会出现「幻肢感觉」（Phantom limb sensation）——一种清晰逼真地感觉已被切除的肢体依然完整存在、甚至正在活动的感觉。更为痛苦的是，许多截肢患者会遭受严重的「幻肢痛」（Phantom limb pain）：真切感受到已被切除的脚趾、脚掌或手部正在经历剧烈的针刺刀割、撕裂性烧灼、扭曲痉挛或压榨样剧痛。幻肢痛绝非患者心理作祟或臆想无病呻吟！它是由中枢大脑感觉皮质重塑以及被离断神经末梢形成创伤性神经瘤向大脑错发神经冲动所致的真实神经病理性疼痛。支持工作者必须坚决共情并承认患者的真实痛楚，严禁轻描淡写地说「肢体都不在了怎么会痛」，及时向护士汇报并协助进行镜像疗法（Mirror therapy）、残端轻柔拍打按摩或神经电刺激止痛。

Prosthetic Device Maintenance and Stump Skin Hygiene

假肢装置清洁维护与残端皮肤卫生保养
A prosthesis is an artificial replacement for a missing body part. The socket of the prosthesis must fit the stump precisely. Skin hygiene is paramount: wash the stump daily with warm water and mild soap; rinse completely to remove all soap residue, which causes contact irritation; pat thoroughly dry before putting on clean stump socks and the prosthesis. Clean the inside of the prosthesis socket daily with a damp cloth and mild cleanser. Regularly inspect the stump skin for blisters, red pressure areas, abrasions, or breakdown; if any skin breakdown is observed, DO NOT apply the prosthesis, and notify the nurse immediately.
假肢（Prosthesis）是替代缺失解剖肢体的精密机械仿生装置。假肢接受腔必须与残端皮肤实现毫米级的完美吻合贴合。残端皮肤卫生是预防感染的重中之重：每日用温水及温和无刺激香皂轻柔清洗残端；反复用清水冲净所有肥皂残留（残留皂基会诱发严重的接触性湿疹皮炎）；用柔软毛巾彻底蘸干后，方可穿戴干净的纯棉残端袜套并套入假肢接受腔。每日用微湿毛巾擦拭假肢接受腔内壁并晾干。每次脱下假肢后，必须严密检查残端皮肤有无发红压痕、摩擦水疱、破损裂口或溃疡；一旦发现任何皮肤破损，严禁强行穿戴假肢，必须立即报告主管护士。

OSTEOARTHRITIS (OA / DEGENERATIVE JOINT DISEASE)

退行性骨关节炎（OA / 骨性关节病）：病理与支持工作要领
Osteoarthritis (OA), also termed degenerative joint disease (DJD), is the most prevalent chronic articular disorder worldwide, strongly linked to aging, wear-and-tear, joint trauma, and obesity. In OA, the protective articular cartilage capping the ends of bones gradually wears away, thins, and frays. Eventually, the exposed rough bone surfaces grind painfully against each other (bone-on-bone friction). In response, the irritated bone ends thicken and produce abnormal bony spurs called osteophytes. OA primarily affects weight-bearing joints (knees, hips, lumbar spine) and distal joints of the fingers. Hallmark symptoms are deep joint aching aggravated by activity, stiffness after inactivity ('gelling' lasting less than 30 minutes upon waking), loss of joint flexibility, crepitus (grating/crackling sounds with movement), and joint enlargement.
退行性骨关节炎（Osteoarthritis，OA），亦称骨性关节病（DJD），是全球发病率最高的老年慢性关节疾病，与增龄老化、机械磨损劳损、关节陈旧外伤及肥胖密切相关。正常关节面表面覆有一层光滑弹性、充当缓冲减震垫的透明软骨。在OA病程中，关节软骨逐渐干燥变薄、龟裂剥脱直至彻底磨损消失。失去了软骨衬垫，粗糙裸露的骨折端在活动时直接发生骨与骨的剧烈硬碰摩擦；作为力学代偿反应，软骨下骨质发生硬化增生，在关节边缘形成尖锐骨刺（骨赘 Osteophytes）。OA主要累及长期承受巨大体重的负重关节（双膝、双髋、下腰椎）以及手指远端关节。典型临床特征为活动后加重的深部骨痛、长时间静止不动后晨起的关节僵硬（「胶着状态」Gelling，通常持续不足30分钟，活动后缓解）、关节活动度受限、活动时发出刺耳的骨擦音/骨碎捻发音（Crepitus）以及骨性粗大畸形。
PAGE 780 · 第 780 页 Heberden's Nodes (Fig 33.12) & Rheumatoid Arthritis (Fig 33.13) / 赫伯登结节（图33.12）与类风湿关节炎病变（图33.13）

OSTEOARTHRITIS VS RHEUMATOID ARTHRITIS (RA)

退行性骨关节炎与类风湿关节炎的鉴别与畸形特征
In osteoarthritis of the hand, bony overgrowths commonly deform the fingers: osteophytic spurs developing on the distal interphalangeal joints (the joints closest to the fingertips) are called Heberden's nodes, while bony spurs on the proximal interphalangeal joints are known as Bouchard's nodes. There is no cure for OA; management focuses on pain relief (acetaminophen, topical NSAIDs), weight management to reduce joint loading, gentle low-impact exercise (swimming, walking), assistive devices (canes, jar openers), and total joint arthroplasty (knee or hip replacement) for end-stage destruction.
在手部骨关节炎中，骨刺增生常导致手指外形发生特征性结节性畸形：发生在手指末节远端指间关节（最靠近指甲末端的关节）的坚硬骨性隆起被称为「赫伯登结节」（Heberden's nodes）；而发生在近端指间关节的骨刺则称为「布夏尔结节」（Bouchard's nodes）。骨关节炎尚无逆转根治方法，临床管理核心在于阶梯止痛（首选对乙酰氨基酚、外用NSAIDs镇痛凝胶）、减重以大幅减轻下肢关节负重应力、低冲击温和运动（游泳、平地散步）、使用省力辅助器具（拐杖、专用开瓶器），并在晚期软骨完全磨灭关节强直时施行全膝或全髋关节置换术。

FIGURE 33.12: Heberden’s nodes. Prominent hard bony spurs and enlargement occurring in the distal interphalangeal (DIP) end joints of the fingers in a client with osteoarthritis.

图 33.12：赫伯登结节（Heberden’s nodes）。严重骨关节炎患者双手指远端指间关节（DIP）因骨刺增生硬化形成的特征性硬质骨性突起与关节膨大畸形。

Rheumatoid Arthritis (RA): Autoimmune Joint Destruction

类风湿关节炎（RA）：系统性自身免疫滑膜炎与进行性畸形
Unlike osteoarthritis (which is a local wear-and-tear condition), Rheumatoid Arthritis (RA) is a chronic, systemic, autoimmune inflammatory disease. The body's immune system mistakenly produces autoantibodies (rheumatoid factor) that attack the synovial membrane lining the joints. This triggers chronic inflammation, synovial hypertrophy (pannus formation), and enzymatic destruction of articular cartilage and underlying bone. RA typically presents symmetrically in smaller joints (wrists, metacarpophalangeal joints, feet) with prolonged morning stiffness lasting greater than 1 hour, joint warmth, spongy swelling, fatigue, low-grade fever, and progressive joint subluxation and severe deformities (ulnar drift, swan-neck deformities, boutonnière deformities).
与单纯局部机械磨损的骨关节炎具有本质区别的是：类风湿关节炎（Rheumatoid Arthritis，RA）是一种全身性、慢性、侵袭性自身免疫性炎症性疾病。机体免疫系统错误产生自身抗体（类风湿因子RF及抗CCP抗体），持续攻击自身关节滑膜组织，诱发慢性滑膜水肿充血、滑膜血管翳（Pannus）过度增生肥厚，释放大量蛋白溶解酶彻底溶解破坏关节软骨、韧带及软骨下骨质。RA具有高度对称性侵犯外周小关节（双侧手腕、掌指关节、双足小关节）的特征，伴随持续超过1小时的严重晨僵、关节红肿热痛、海绵样梭形肿胀、周身低热与重度疲劳；晚期导致严重的关节半脱位与永久性畸形（如尺侧偏斜、天鹅颈样畸形及纽扣花样畸形）。

FIGURE 33.13: Severe hand deformities caused by advanced rheumatoid arthritis, featuring marked ulnar deviation of the fingers, metacarpophalangeal subluxation, and joint destruction.

图 33.13：晚期类风湿关节炎引发的严重手部毁损性畸形，呈现极为典型的掌指关节半脱位破坏及全部手指朝向小指侧的特征性「尺侧偏斜畸形」（Ulnar drift）。

PAGE 781 · 第 781 页 Osteoporosis, Gout (Podagra) & Fibromyalgia Management / 骨质疏松脆性骨折、痛风石急性发作与纤维肌痛管理

OSTEOPOROSIS: THE SILENT BONE THIEF

骨质疏松症（Osteoporosis）：隐匿性骨量流失与脆性骨折
Osteoporosis literally means 'porous bone' (osteo = bone, porosis = porous). It is a progressive metabolic bone disease in which bone resorption by osteoclasts outpaces bone formation by osteoblasts. The internal trabecular meshwork of the bone becomes thin, porous, and brittle, drastically reducing bone strength. Osteoporosis is called a 'silent disease' because bone loss occurs without any pain or symptoms for decades until a bone breaks. Fractures commonly occur in the hip, vertebrae (causing loss of height and severe spinal curvature known as kyphosis or 'dowager's hump'), and wrist. Risk factors include advanced age, female sex, postmenopausal estrogen deficiency, low calcium/vitamin D intake, lack of weight-bearing exercise, smoking, and long-term corticosteroid use. Management involves adequate calcium and vitamin D supplementation, fall-prevention measures, and prescription bisphosphonate medications.
骨质疏松症（Osteoporosis）字面释义为「多孔骨骼」（osteo=骨，porosis=多孔多穴）。这是一种全身代谢性骨病，其特征是破骨细胞引起的骨吸收速度远远超过成骨细胞的骨形成速度，导致骨量不可逆流失、骨微小结构网架严重变薄变脆，骨生物力学校准强度锐减。骨质疏松被称为「无声的隐形杀手」，因为骨量在长达数十年的流失过程中完全没有任何疼痛体征，绝大多数长者直至发生骨折才被确诊。最易发生脆性骨折的解剖部位为股骨颈、脊柱胸腰椎（椎体受压发生楔形压缩，导致身高缩短数厘米并形成特征性的驼背畸形即「富贵包/寡妇驼背」Dowager's hump）以及桡骨远端手腕。危险因素包括高龄、绝经后雌激素断崖式下跌、钙与维生素D摄入匮乏、缺乏负重运动、吸烟酗酒及长期服用糖皮质激素。照护要点包括科学补充钙质与活性维生素D、全面落实防跌倒设施以及规范遵医嘱使用双膦酸盐抑制骨吸收药物。

Gout (Metabolic Arthritis) & Purine Dietary Control

痛风（高尿酸血症性关节炎）与嘌呤饮食控制
Gout is a painful inflammatory metabolic arthritis caused by hyperuricemia (excess uric acid in the blood). Uric acid is a normal byproduct of purine metabolism. When uric acid levels exceed saturation, needle-sharp monosodium urate crystals precipitate into joint cartilage, synovial fluid, and tendons. In about 75% to 80% of acute presentations, gout attacks the metatarsophalangeal joint of the great toe (a condition known as podagra). The onset is sudden, often waking the client at night with excruciating, burning, throbbing pain where even the weight of a bedsheet is agonizing. The joint is swollen, shiny, red, and hot to the touch. In chronic gout, hard crystalline deposits called tophi form in the ears, knuckles, and elbows. Care involves elevating the joint, keeping heavy blankets off the foot using a bed cradle, administering anti-inflammatory/colchicine medications, encouraging high fluid intake (2 to 3 litres daily) to flush uric acid through kidneys, and avoiding high-purine foods (organ meats, anchovies, shellfish, red meat, beer, and high-fructose corn syrup).
痛风（Gout）是因体内嘌呤代谢紊乱导致血尿酸水平持续过高（高尿酸血症）所致的代谢性炎症性关节炎。当血液尿酸浓度达到超饱和状态时，细长如针的微细单钠尿酸盐结晶在关节滑膜、关节软骨及肌腱鞘内析出沉淀。在约75%至80%的初次急性发作中，痛风首发侵犯单侧大脚趾的第一跖趾关节（医学特称为足痛风 Podagra）。发作极其迅猛，常在夜间熟睡中因刀割样、火烧样剧痛突发惊醒，病变关节极度敏感，连轻微触碰甚至一张棉被盖上去都会引发撕心裂肺的剧痛；局部关节肿胀发亮、色泽暗红且皮温灼热。在慢性痛风患者中，尿酸结晶在耳郭软骨、指间关节及鹰嘴滑囊堆积形成石灰样硬块结节，即「痛风石」（Tophi）。照护要领：患肢抬高制动；使用床档支架（Bed cradle）将厚重被褥撑起，绝对严禁压迫患趾；遵医嘱给予消炎止痛药或秋水仙碱；鼓励每日大量饮水（2000至3000毫升）以碱化尿液促进尿酸从肾脏排泄；严格指导长者恪守低嘌呤饮食（严禁动物内脏、凤尾鱼、沙丁鱼、甲壳海鲜、浓肉汤、啤酒及高果糖饮料）。

Fibromyalgia Syndrome: Widespread Musculoskeletal Pain

纤维肌痛综合征：全身性中枢敏化与慢性广泛疼痛
Fibromyalgia is a chronic neurological pain syndrome characterized by widespread aching, stiffness, and severe tender points across muscles, ligaments, and tendons, accompanied by profound fatigue, non-restorative sleep, and cognitive challenges ('fibro fog'). It is now understood as a central pain amplification disorder (central sensitization), where the brain and spinal cord amplify sensory pain signals. It does not cause permanent joint damage. Support workers assist by promoting gentle low-impact exercises, warm baths, consistent sleep routines, stress reduction, and compassionate emotional validation.
纤维肌痛综合征（Fibromyalgia）是一种以全身对称性弥漫性骨骼肌软组织慢性疼痛、广泛压痛点、晨起僵硬、深度不可缓解性疲劳、非恢复性睡眠障碍以及认知迟钝（「纤维雾」Fibro fog）为特征的慢性神经功能障碍综合征。现代神经病理学证实其本质是中枢神经系统痛觉过敏敏化放大障碍，大脑和脊髓将普通轻微触碰错误放大为剧烈疼痛信号。该病不会引起永久性骨骼关节器质性破坏。支持工作者协助要点：鼓励并陪伴患者进行水中温和太极、平地行走等低冲击运动；指导睡前温水泡浴缓解肌肉紧张；建立规律就寝作息；协助减压并给予充满同理心的心理安抚。
PAGE 782 · 第 782 页 Nervous System Overview, Epilepsy & Seizure First Aid / 神经系统疾病总论、癫痫发作分型与安全急救红线

NERVOUS SYSTEM DISORDERS: EPILEPSY AND SEIZURES

神经系统疾患：癫痫电生理机制与发作分类
The nervous system controls all sensory perception, cognitive thought, motor coordination, and vital autonomic involuntary functions. Neurological disorders can originate from prenatal brain malformations, traumatic brain injury (blows to head, falls, gunshots, vehicular collisions), cerebrovascular disruption (strokes), central nervous system infections (meningitis, encephalitis), toxic metabolic imbalances, or progressive neurodegenerative processes. Epilepsy: Epilepsy is a neurological disorder characterized by recurrent, unprovoked seizures caused by sudden, abnormal, excessive, and synchronized electrical discharges across cerebral neurons. Seizures present across a clinical spectrum: • Focal Seizures (Partial Seizures): Originate in a localized region of one cerebral hemisphere. May cause localized motor twitching, sensory tingling, auditory/visual hallucinations, or altered awareness without complete collapse. • Generalized Seizures: Involve both cerebral hemispheres simultaneously: - Absence Seizures (Petit Mal): Brief, sudden lapses in consciousness lasting 5 to 15 seconds where the client stares blankly into space without motor collapse, frequently mistaken for daydreaming. - Tonic-Clonic Seizures (Grand Mal): The most dramatic presentation. Progresses through a Tonic phase (sudden loss of consciousness, body stiffens rigidly, breath holding with cyanosis) followed immediately by a Clonic phase (violent, rhythmic, jerking muscle contractions of the entire body, jaw clenching, salivation/frothing at the mouth, urinary incontinence). Seizures typically last 1 to 3 minutes, followed by a Postictal phase of deep exhaustion, confusion, headache, and prolonged sleep.
中枢及周围神经系统统筹掌管人体全部感觉感知、思维记忆、运动随意协调以及维持生命的非随意自主神经功能。神经系统器质性疾患可由胚胎期脑发育异常、颅脑外伤（跌倒撞击、枪击伤、车祸脑挫裂伤）、脑血管循环中断（脑卒中）、中枢感染（脑膜炎、脑炎）、代谢中毒性紊乱以及慢性进行性神经退行性病变引发。 癫痫（Epilepsy）：癫痫是一种由大脑皮层神经元群突发、过度、高同步化异常病理性放电引起的慢性复发性短暂脑功能失调综合征。临床表现形式极为多样： • 局灶性发作（部分性发作）：起源于单侧大脑半球的局限性脑区。可表现为单侧面部或肢体局限性抽动、局部肢体麻木刺痛、视幻觉/听幻觉，或伴有自动症等意识改变，但不一定出现全身抽搐倒地。 • 全面性发作：两半球神经元同步广泛异常放电： - 失神发作（小发作 Petit Mal）：突发而短暂的意识丧失中断，持续仅5至15秒；长者或儿童突发两眼凝视发呆、动作瞬间停顿、呼之不应，事后无记忆，常被误认为是发呆走神。 - 强直-阵挛发作（大发作 Grand Mal / Tonic-Clonic）：临床最为危重震撼的发作类型。首先进入强直期（突发意识丧失倒地摔伤、全身骨骼肌强直性痉挛僵硬、呼吸肌痉挛屏气面色发绀青紫），随后立即转入阵挛期（全身肌肉发生节律性、剧烈、抽搐性抽动、牙关紧闭、口吐白沫带有血沫、大小便失禁）。抽搐发作通常持续1至3分钟，随后进入发作后期（Postictal phase），患者极度疲乏嗜睡、头痛恶心、意识朦胧混乱持续数小时。

Seizure First Aid and Support Worker Safety Protocols

癫痫发作现场急救与支持工作者安全保护红线
When a client experiences a generalized tonic-clonic seizure, the support worker must act calmly to protect the client from physical injury: 1. Stay with the client and note the time the seizure began. 2. Lower the client gently to the floor if they are seated or standing, to prevent a traumatic fall. 3. Protect the head: Place a soft pillow, folded jacket, or your hands beneath the client’s head. 4. Clear the immediate area of hard, sharp, or hot objects (furniture, glasses, radiators) to prevent trauma during violent jerking. 5. Loosen tight clothing around the neck (ties, buttons, collars) to facilitate breathing. 6. DO NOT RESTRAIN the client: Never hold down their arms or legs or try to stop the jerking movements; fighting against the contractions can cause joint dislocations, muscle tears, or bone fractures. 7. ABSOLUTE MEDICAL RED LINE: NEVER put anything in the client’s mouth! Do not insert bite blocks, spoons, wooden rulers, or your fingers. It is anatomically impossible for a person to swallow their own tongue! Forcing objects into the mouth fractures teeth, causes jaw dislocation, severely lacerates the tongue, and creates loose tooth fragments that can aspirate into the lungs and cause fatal airway obstruction. 8. Turn the client onto their side (recovery position) as soon as it is safe to do so, allowing saliva, blood, and vomitus to drain out of the mouth, keeping the airway clear. 9. Call 911 / Emergency Services if: the seizure lasts longer than 5 minutes (status epilepticus), a second seizure begins immediately after the first, the client does not regain consciousness, the seizure occurs in water, or the client is injured or pregnant.
当服务对象突发全身强直-阵挛性癫痫抽搐时，支持工作者必须保持冷静，分秒必争采取保护性急救措施，防止继发创伤： 1. 绝不可离开患者：全程守在患者身旁，立即看表记录发作开始的精确时间。 2. 顺势放倒协助平卧：若患者正坐在椅子上或站立，顺势平稳将其滑移放倒至地面，严防直立倒地造成颅骨骨折与严重摔伤。 3. 保护头部：立即将折叠的衣物、软枕或您的双手垫在患者头颅下方，避免头部反复剧烈撞击硬质地面造成脑挫伤。 4. 清理周围环境：迅速移开周围一切桌椅坚硬家具、玻璃杯、锐器或发热暖气片，防止肢体剧烈挥舞抽搐时被划伤或烫伤。 5. 解开颈部紧身衣物：松解领带、解开衬衫领扣与皮带，保持呼吸道畅通。 6. 严禁强行按压束缚肢体：绝不可用身体重量压制或强行按压拉扯抽搐的四肢，对抗强大的肌肉痉挛极易导致关节脱位、肌肉肌腱撕裂甚至长骨骨折。 7. 绝对医学急救红线：严禁向口中塞入任何物品！绝不可强行向患者牙齿间硬塞压舌板、金属勺子、筷子或您自己的手指！解剖学上舌头受舌骨和舌系带牵拉，任何人绝不可能把自己的舌头「吞进喉咙」！强行撬嘴塞硬物会撬断牙齿、造成下颌骨脱臼、严重割伤舌头，脱落的碎牙还会被误吸入气管引发致命性呼吸道窒息！ 8. 发作后协助侧卧（恢复体位）：抽搐减弱或停止后，立即将患者身体和头面整体平移侧卧（复原体位），使口腔内的分泌物、唾液及呕吐物流出嘴角，防止倒吸窒息。 9. 呼叫急救医疗（911）指征：抽搐持续超过5分钟未见缓解（癫痫持续状态，极度危及生命）、抽搐连续发作意识未恢复、发作发生在水中、患者有明显外伤或为孕妇时，必须立即呼叫救护车。
PAGE 783 · 第 783 页 Stroke (CVA) Pathology, Brain Mapping & FAST Protocol / 脑卒中（脑血管意外）病理、脑机能定位与 FAST 急救预警

STROKE (CEREBROVASCULAR ACCIDENT / CVA)

脑卒中（脑中风 / 脑血管意外）：病理机制与分类
A stroke, medically termed a Cerebrovascular Accident (CVA), occurs when the arterial blood supply to a specific region of the brain is suddenly interrupted, or when a cerebral blood vessel ruptures. Brain tissue is entirely dependent on a continuous supply of oxygen and glucose carried in blood; without blood flow, brain cells begin dying within 3 to 4 minutes. A stroke is an acute medical emergency. Two Fundamental Types of Stroke: 1. Ischemic Stroke: Accounts for approximately 80% to 85% of all strokes. Occurs when an artery supplying the brain becomes blocked by a blood clot. Can be Thrombotic (a clot / thrombus forms directly over an atherosclerotic plaque in a cerebral artery) or Embolic (a blood clot, plaque fragment, or cardiac thrombus forms elsewhere—frequently in the fibrillating left atrium of the heart in atrial fibrillation—breaks off, travels through circulation, and lodges in a smaller cerebral artery). 2. Hemorrhagic Stroke: Accounts for 15% to 20% of strokes. Occurs when a weakened cerebral blood vessel bursts, leaking blood directly into surrounding brain parenchyma (intracerebral hemorrhage) or the subarachnoid space (subarachnoid hemorrhage). Blood pooling increases intracranial pressure (ICP), compresses brain structures, and causes widespread cell death. Common causes are chronic uncontrolled hypertension and ruptured cerebral aneurysms.
脑卒中（Stroke），医学专业术语称为脑血管意外（Cerebrovascular Accident，CVA），是指脑部动脉血液供应突发中断，或脑血管破裂出血导致局部脑组织缺血坏死的一组急性脑血管疾病。中枢脑神经元细胞对缺血缺氧极度敏感，完全依赖血流源源不断输送氧气和葡萄糖；脑血流一旦中断，神经细胞在3至4分钟内便开始发生不可逆坏死。脑卒中属于分秒必争的神经科最危急重症。 脑卒中的两大基本病理类型： 1. 缺血性脑卒中（脑梗死）：占全部卒中病例的80%至85%左右。系供应脑组织的动脉被血栓机械性完全阻塞所致。细分为脑血栓形成（Thrombotic——脑动脉粥样硬化斑块破裂在局部原位迅速继发血栓形成闭塞管腔）与脑栓塞（Embolic——身体其他部位如心房颤动时左心房脱落的附壁血栓、颈动脉斑块碎片随血液流动冲入脑动脉并嵌顿于管径狭窄的脑微小血管）。 2. 出血性脑卒中（脑出血）：占卒中病例的15%至20%。系颅内病变脑动脉突发破裂，大量血液喷涌进入脑实质形成血肿（脑实质出血）或流入蛛网膜下隙（蛛网膜下腔出血）。血肿不仅直接破坏邻近神经束，还会导致颅内压（ICP）急剧恶性升高、脑组织移位形成脑疝直接危及生命。最主要的诱因为长期未受控的高血压硬化动脉以及先天性颅内动脉瘤（Aneurysm）或动静脉畸形破裂。

Brain Functional Mapping and Loss of Function

大脑功能受损定位图谱：损伤区域与神经功能缺失对应关系
Because motor nerve tracts cross (decussate) in the medulla of the brainstem, damage to the right cerebral hemisphere results in neuromuscular deficits on the left side of the body, whereas damage to the left cerebral hemisphere results in deficits on the right side of the body. Specific functional deficits depend precisely on which brain lobe and vascular territory is injured: • Frontal Lobe Damage: Loss of voluntary motor function (paralysis, hemiplegia), expressive motor speech deficits (Broca’s aphasia), cognitive and executive dysfunction, personality changes, impulsivity, and emotional lability (sudden, unpredictable laughing or crying). • Parietal Lobe Damage: Loss of sensory perception (numbness, hemisensory loss), inability to recognize objects by touch (astereognosis), and spatial neglect (unilateral neglect of the affected side). • Temporal Lobe Damage: Receptive language comprehension deficits (Wernicke’s aphasia, where speech is fluent but incomprehensible), and severe memory impairments. • Occipital Lobe Damage: Visual field deficits, including homonymous hemianopia (blindness in half of the visual field of both eyes).
由于大脑皮层发出的锥体束运动神经传导纤维在脑干延髓部位发生交叉下行（延髓交叉），右侧大脑半球受损直接导致左侧身体肌肉运动瘫痪与感觉障碍；而左侧大脑半球受损则引起右侧身体肢体偏瘫。具体的神经系统缺损特征精准取决于受损脑叶与缺血血管供血区： • 额叶（Frontal Lobe）受损：随意运动中枢破坏导致偏瘫（Hemiplegia）、运动性失语症（Broca失语——能听懂但无法用语言表达，言语断续吃力）、执行认知障碍、冲动攻击行为、以及特征性的「情绪失禁」（Emotional lability——毫无诱因突发嚎啕大哭或大笑）。 • 顶叶（Parietal Lobe）受损：躯体感觉中枢破坏引起对侧肢体深浅感觉丧失（偏侧麻木）、实体辨别觉丧失（闭眼无法用手辨识硬币），以及单侧空间忽略（完全忽视患侧肢体及视野）。 • 颞叶（Temporal Lobe）受损：感觉性语言理解中枢破坏（Wernicke失语——听不懂别人在说什么，自身语流连贯但词不达意不知所云），以及严重的近期记忆丧失。 • 枕叶（Occipital Lobe）受损：视觉皮层损伤导致视野缺损，最常见为同向偏盲（双眼同侧半边视野完全失明）。

FIGURE 33.14: Functional mapping of the cerebral cortex. Neurological deficits experienced after a stroke correspond directly to the anatomical area of brain tissue destroyed by ischemia or hemorrhage.

图 33.14：大脑皮层解剖功能映射图。脑卒中发作后患者呈现的各项神经功能障碍，与缺血坏死或出血破坏的大脑皮层特定功能区（运动区、感觉区、语言言语中枢及视觉区）具有严格的病理对应关系。

Recognizing Stroke: The FAST Protocol and Emergency Activation

识别脑卒中黄金预警：FAST 急救筛查法则与救治时效
Time is brain! For ischemic strokes, intravenous thrombolytic 'clot-busting' medications (tissue plasminogen activator / tPA) must be administered within a strict 4.5-hour therapeutic window from symptom onset to dissolve the clot and salvage penumbra brain tissue. The Heart and Stroke Foundation of Canada promotes the FAST mnemonic for immediate recognition: • F - FACE: Is it drooping? Ask the person to smile. Does one side of the face droop downward or appear numb? • A - ARMS: Can you raise both? Ask the person to raise both arms. Does one arm drift downward or feel weak and clumsy? • S - SPEECH: Is it slurred or jumbled? Ask the person to repeat a simple sentence. Can they speak clearly, or do words sound slurred, garbled, or completely absent? • T - TIME: Time to call 911 immediately! If the client demonstrates ANY of these signs, do not wait, do not call family first, and do not drive them yourself. Call 911 immediately and tell the dispatcher: 'I suspect a stroke.' Note the exact time symptoms were first observed.
时间就是大脑，时间就是生命！在缺血性脑卒中发生时，静脉溶栓药物（重组组织型纤溶酶原激活剂 tPA）必须在症状初发的「4.5小时黄金时间窗」内静脉推注，以溶解微血栓、恢复缺血半暗带脑血流灌注并挽救濒死神经细胞。加拿大心脏与卒中基金会确立了全球通用的 FAST 脑卒中快速筛查法则： • F（Face 面部面瘫）：面部是否对称有无下垂？请长者露齿微笑。观察是否有一侧嘴角下垂、鼻唇沟变浅或口角漏水？ • A（Arms 上肢无力）：双臂能否平举？请长者向前平举双臂10秒。观察是否有一侧手臂无力下垂掉落？ • S（Speech 言语障碍）：说话是否含糊不清或语无伦次？请长者复述一句简单的话。观察说话是否吐字不清、发音含糊、大舌头或完全丧失语言能力？ • T（Time 抢救时间就是生命）：立即呼叫 911 急救电话！一旦发现上述任何一项体征突发出现，切勿迟疑观望！切勿先联系非急救亲友！切勿自行驾车送医！立即拨打 911，向调度员明确报告「怀疑急性脑卒中」，并精确告知症状最早出现的具体时间点！
PAGE 784 · 第 784 页 Stroke Rehabilitation, Hemiplegia & Communication Care / 脑卒中康复护理：偏瘫照料、吞咽防误吸与失语沟通

STROKE REHABILITATION AND COMPREHENSIVE CLIENT SUPPORT

脑卒中后期康复支持与生活自理能力重塑
Following acute hospital stabilization, most stroke survivors return to the community requiring specialized home care or transition into subacute rehabilitation or long-term care facilities. The primary goal of rehabilitation is to help the client regain highest possible functional independence, prevent complications of immobility, and adapt to permanent neurological deficits. Support workers play an indispensable role in daily restorative care: • Supporting Hemiplegia & ADLs: Always dress the affected (weaker) side first, and undress the unaffected (stronger) side first. Position the client in optimal anatomical alignment in bed and chair to prevent hip external rotation and shoulder subluxation. When transferring, position the wheelchair on the client's stronger side so they lead with their strong leg and arm. • Dysphagia & Aspiration Prevention: Dysphagia (difficulty swallowing) affects over 50% of stroke clients due to cranial nerve weakness. Aspiration of food or fluids into the lungs can cause fatal pneumonia. Guidelines: Keep the client sitting upright at a 90-degree angle during meals and for at least 30 to 60 minutes after eating; provide prescribed food textures (pureed, minced, or soft) and thickened liquids (nectar, honey, or pudding thick); feed small bites slowly into the unaffected side of the mouth; verify that the client has swallowed each bite and inspect for pocketing of food in the cheek on the paralyzed side. • Communicating with Aphasic Clients: Aphasia is the partial or complete loss of the ability to articulate or comprehend language. Speak slowly, clearly, and in a normal tone of voice (do not shout). Use short, simple sentences; allow ample time (10 to 15 seconds) for the client to process and respond; use yes/no questions; utilize communication boards, picture cards, gestures, and written cues; never finish their sentences impatiently.
经过急性期医院抢救生命体征平稳后，大多数脑卒中幸存者转入社区接受居家支持，或进入亚急性康复中心及长期照护机构。卒中康复的核心宗旨是最大限度促进神经功能重塑、代偿重获生活自理能力，并预防废用综合征及继发并发症。支持工作者在患者日常功能维持中发挥着不可替代的关键作用： • 偏瘫肢体照护与ADL协助原则：协助穿衣时，必须遵循「先穿患侧（瘫痪侧）、后穿健侧；脱衣时先脱健侧、后脱患侧」的核心原则。卧床或坐轮椅时，务必维持患肢处于抗痉挛良肢位，防止患侧肩关节半脱位及足下垂。床椅转移时，将轮椅置于长者的「健侧」，使长者用健侧肢体作为主力发力支撑旋转。 • 吞咽困难（Dysphagia）与防误吸硬红线：超过半数的卒中幸存者因延髓麻痹伴有吞咽神经肌肉协调障碍。食物或液体一旦误吸入气管肺部，会诱发极为凶险的吸入性肺炎甚至窒息死亡。照护规程：进餐全程必须使长者保持90度完全端坐位，餐后保持坐位至少30至60分钟；严格遵言语治疗师医嘱提供指定质地的糊状/细碎软食以及增稠液体（花蜜级、蜂蜜级或布丁级稠度）；每次喂食极小勺，将食物送入其口内未瘫痪的「健侧颊部」；确保咽下一口后再喂下一口，进餐完毕仔细检查并清除瘫痪侧面颊内蓄积残留的食物残渣（防颊囊蓄食）。 • 失语症（Aphasia）沟通支持技巧：失语症是指大脑语言中枢受损导致理解或表达语言能力的部分或完全丧失。沟通要领：语速放慢、发音清晰、语气温和自然（切勿大声吼叫）；使用简短明确的短句；每次提问后给予充足的思考反应时间（耐心理智等待10至15秒）；尽量设计可用「是/否」或点头摇头回答的封闭式问题；充分借助图文沟通板、照片卡片、直观肢体手势辅助；耐性倾听，绝不可在长者艰难表达时急躁地抢话打断。

Emotional Lability and Post-Stroke Depression

卒中后情绪失禁（假性球麻痹情绪）与抑郁干预
Many stroke clients experience emotional lability (pseudobulbar affect)—sudden, uncontrollable outbursts of laughing or crying that are completely disproportionate or inappropriate to the actual emotional state. The client may burst into loud sobs while trying to say good morning, or laugh uncontrollably during a serious moment. The client cannot control these outbursts and is often deeply embarrassed. The support worker must remain calm, validate that this is a physical neurological symptom caused by brain damage, avoid scolding or acting shocked, and gently redirect the client's attention. Post-stroke depression is also very common, requiring vigilant monitoring and reporting of changes in mood or appetite.
许多卒中患者在康复期会出现令人困惑的「情绪失禁」（Emotional lability / 假性球麻痹情绪表露）：患者会在完全没有明显外部悲伤或喜悦诱因的情况下，突发无法自控的嚎啕大哭或狂笑不止。长者可能在试图礼貌打招呼时突然失声痛哭，或在严肃时刻难以自制地大笑。患者内心完全清楚这一反应与自身真实情感严重脱节，事后往往极度羞愧沮丧。支持工作者必须保持温和镇定，向家属和长者明确解释这是大脑神经调控中枢受损的器质性物理体征而非患者故意为之，绝不可表现出震惊或责怪，可通过温和变换话题分散注意力。此外，卒中后抑郁（PSD）发生率高达三分之一以上，需密切观察患者情绪低落、悲观绝望或拒绝进食等异常行为并即时上报。
PAGE 785 · 第 785 页 Acquired Brain Injury (ABI), TBI & Cognitive Care / 获得性脑损伤（ABI/TBI）：创伤机制、认知功能丧失与行为干预

ACQUIRED BRAIN INJURY (ABI) AND TRAUMATIC BRAIN INJURY (TBI)

获得性脑损伤（ABI）与创伤性脑损伤（TBI）：神经创伤病理
An Acquired Brain Injury (ABI) is damage to brain tissue that occurs after birth and is not related to a congenital disorder or a degenerative disease. ABIs are categorized into Traumatic Brain Injury (TBI, resulting from external mechanical forces such as vehicular crashes, severe falls, sports collisions, blows, or gunshot assault) and Non-Traumatic Brain Injury (resulting from internal vascular or medical events, including strokes, brain tumours, near-drowning hypoxia, cardiac arrest anoxia, carbon monoxide poisoning, or central nervous system infections like meningitis and encephalitis). Biomechanics of TBI: When the head is subjected to violent acceleration-deceleration or rotational forces, the soft, gelatinous brain is slammed forcefully against the hard internal ridges of the bony skull (coup-contrecoup injury). This impact tears cerebral blood vessels (causing epidural, subdural, or intracerebral hematomas), bruises brain tissue (contusion), and stretches and shears microscopic axonal connections throughout the white matter (diffuse axonal injury / DAI), resulting in massive intracranial swelling, cerebral edema, and neuronal death.
获得性脑损伤（Acquired Brain Injury，ABI）是指在个体出生之后发生的、排除先天性畸形或遗传退行性疾病所致的脑组织器质性损害。ABI分为两大核心范畴：创伤性脑损伤（TBI——由外部机械物理暴力引发，如车祸剧烈撞击、高处坠落、剧烈运动冲撞、头部重击或枪击伤）以及非创伤性脑损伤（由体内血管或内科事件引发，包括脑卒中、颅内肿瘤侵犯、溺水窒息缺氧、心跳骤停缺血缺氧性脑病、一氧化碳中毒以及脑膜炎、脑炎等中枢感染）。 创伤性脑损伤生物力学：当头部遭受剧烈加速-减速或旋转剪切暴力时，柔软如凝胶状的大脑组织在坚硬且内壁粗糙不平的颅骨内壁发生剧烈撞击回弹（冲击伤与对冲伤 Coup-contrecoup injury）。剧烈碰撞导致脑表面血管破裂形成硬膜外、硬膜下或脑内血肿，造成脑挫裂伤，并引起广泛神经轴突牵拉撕裂剪切破坏（弥漫性轴索损伤 DAI），诱发全脑组织恶性水肿和颅内压急剧增高，导致神经元大面积坏死。

Cognitive, Behavioral and Emotional Manifestations of ABI

脑损伤后的认知障碍、行为改变与结构化环境支持
Surviving clients with moderate to severe ABI often experience profound, long-term multi-domain impairments that completely alter their personality and daily living capacity: • Cognitive Deficits: Severe short-term memory loss (inability to recall events from 5 minutes ago, while remote memories remain intact), poor attention span, slow mental processing speed, executive dysfunction (inability to plan, sequence, or solve simple problems), and lack of insight into their own disabilities (anosognosia). • Behavioral & Personality Disruptions: Frontal lobe disinhibition causes impulsivity, loss of social tact, inappropriate sexual remarks or disrobing, explosive anger over minor frustrations, aggression, restlessness, and profound apathy. • Sensory-Motor Deficits: Hemiplegia, ataxia (uncoordinated movement), tremors, persistent headaches, visual disturbances, and sensory overload from noise or bright lights. Support Worker Guidelines for ABI Care: 1. Predictable Structured Routine: Maintain a strict, unchanging daily schedule for meals, ADLs, and rest. Predictability reduces cognitive anxiety and catastrophic behavioural outbursts. 2. Environmental De-escalation: Keep the physical environment quiet, calm, and decluttered. Minimize loud televisions, chaotic group activities, and bright lights that trigger sensory overload. 3. Memory Prompts: Use large wall calendars, daily checklists, clear signage, and consistent object placement to support impaired memory. 4. De-escalating Agitation: If the client becomes agitated, never argue or shout back. Stay calm, lower your voice, validate their feelings, reduce environmental stimuli, and gently redirect them toward a calming, familiar activity.
中重度脑损伤幸存者通常面临广泛、深远且伴随终生的多维度功能缺损，往往使其人格性格与自主生活能力发生颠覆性改变： • 认知功能障碍：严重的近事记忆力减退（能清晰回忆几十年前的往事，却完全记不得5分钟前刚吃过的餐食或刚见过的人）、注意力无法集中涣散、信息加工反应极度迟缓、执行功能丧失（无法规划或执行步骤化的简单任务），以及对自身病残完全缺乏自知力（Anosognosia）。 • 人格行为与情绪失控：额叶受损导致中枢抑制功能完全丧失，患者常表现出缺乏社交分寸的冲动行为、言语污秽轻浮、当众脱衣等不当性行为；遇轻微挫折即暴跳如雷甚至出现攻击性破坏行为；或走向另一个极端，表现出极度冷漠麻木、丧失一切主动性。 • 感觉运动协调障碍：偏瘫、共济失调（动作笨拙蹒跚）、意向性震颤、慢性顽固性头痛、畏光畏声，对外界嘈杂刺激极易产生感觉超载。 支持工作者照护 ABI 患者的核心实操准则： 1. 建立高度一致的结构化作息：每日起床、就餐、个人护理和休息严格按固定日程推行。可预测的结构化环境能极大减轻患者的认知焦虑，预防灾难性情绪爆发。 2. 营造低刺激宁静环境：保持居室环境整洁安宁。调低电视音量、避免群体嘈杂围观和刺眼灯光，防范感觉超载诱发烦躁。 3. 充分借助具象记忆提示工具：在房间醒目位置张贴大字大日历、步骤清晰的每日任务核对清单、门牌图文标识，并将生活用品固定放置于恒定位置。 4. 冲动激惹行为的去升级化化解：当长者烦躁激动时，绝对严禁与其争辩、训斥或硬碰对抗！工作人员必须保持温和从容、放低语调、承认其情绪体验，迅速撤除周围刺激源，极其轻柔地引导其转移注意力到熟悉的舒缓活动中。
PAGE 786 · 第 786 页 Spinal Cord Injury (SCI): Paraplegia vs Quadriplegia / 脊髓损伤（SCI）：截瘫与高位四肢瘫的神经解剖定位

SPINAL CORD INJURY (SCI): ANATOMICAL LEVELS AND PARALYSIS

脊髓损伤（SCI）：神经解剖损伤节段与瘫痪范围
The spinal cord is the main neural trunk transmitting motor commands from the brain to the peripheral body and relaying sensory feedback from the body back to the brain. A Spinal Cord Injury (SCI) damages the spinal cord tissue within the vertebral canal, resulting in partial or complete loss of motor function (paralysis), sensation, and autonomic control below the level of the injury. Most SCIs occur in young adult males from motor vehicle crashes, sporting accidents (diving into shallow water), falls, and violence. Paralysis Classification Based on Injury Level: • Cervical Spine Injuries (C1 to C8) — Quadriplegia (Tetraplegia): Paralysis of all four extremities (both arms and both legs) as well as the trunk and pelvic organs. Injuries at C1 to C3 destroy nerve supply to the diaphragm (phrenic nerve), resulting in immediate fatal respiratory arrest unless the client is permanently maintained on a mechanical ventilator. Injuries at C4 to C8 allow varying degrees of neck, shoulder, and arm function, but lower limbs and autonomic bowel/bladder control remain completely paralyzed. • Thoracic, Lumbar, or Sacral Injuries (T1 to S5) — Paraplegia: Paralysis and loss of sensation affecting the lower half of the body (both legs and parts of the trunk). Arm, neck, and shoulder function are fully preserved, enabling paraplegic clients to become highly proficient in independent wheelchair transfers and upper-body sports.
脊髓是连接大脑与外周神经系统的主干通路，负责向下传导大脑的随意运动指令，并向上传导全身皮肤内脏的感觉信息。脊髓损伤（Spinal Cord Injury，SCI）是椎管内脊髓受到骨折脱位骨片、韧带血肿压迫或挫裂伤，导致损伤神经节段平面以下的感觉传导完全或部分阻断、随意运动功能丧失（瘫痪）以及自主神经反射功能完全丧失。脊髓损伤多见于青年男性，最常见由车祸碰撞、高处坠落、潜水跳入浅水头部撞底以及体育暴力创伤引起。 依据脊髓解剖损伤节段的瘫痪分型： • 颈髓节段损伤（C1至C8）—— 四肢瘫（Quadriplegia / Tetraplegia）：表现为双上肢、双下肢全部四肢瘫痪，伴躯干肌肉及盆腔脏器功能完全丧失。C1至C3节段损伤直接摧毁了支配膈肌的膈神经运动中枢，患者在受伤瞬间立即发生致命性呼吸停止，必须终身依赖呼吸机机械通气维持生命；C4至C8节段损伤虽可保留部分头颈活动、耸肩或不同程度的手臂屈肘伸腕动作，但双下肢完全瘫痪，大小便完全失禁。 • 胸髓、腰髓或骶髓节段损伤（T1至S5）—— 截瘫（Paraplegia）：表现为身体下半部（双下肢及部分下部躯干）运动麻痹与深浅感觉丧失。双上肢、肩带及颈胸部肌肉功能完全完好无损，经过系统康复训练，截瘫患者能够极其熟练地运用上肢肌力完成床-轮椅自主转移并具备高度的生活自理能力。

Complications of SCI: Autonomic Dysreflexia and Neurogenic Bladder

脊髓损伤严重并发症：自主神经反射异常与神经源性膀胱
Clients living with chronic spinal cord injury face life-threatening clinical complications requiring vigilant support worker oversight: • Autonomic Dysreflexia (Hyperreflexia): An acute, life-threatening medical emergency affecting clients with spinal cord injuries at or above the T6 level. Triggered by painful sensory stimuli below the level of injury—most commonly an overdistended, full bladder (blocked Foley catheter), severe bowel fecal impaction, or skin pressure sores. The spinal cord below the lesion triggers massive, uncontrolled sympathetic nervous system reflex firing, causing severe arterial vasoconstriction. Symptoms: Sudden, explosive, dangerous rise in blood pressure (systolic often exceeding 200 to 300 mm Hg), pounding headache, profuse sweating and flushing above the injury level, and cold, pale goosebumps below the injury level. This hypertensive crisis can cause fatal stroke, seizure, or cardiac arrest within minutes! Emergency Action: Sit the client immediately upright at 90 degrees (causes orthostatic blood pooling to lower cerebral pressure), notify the nurse immediately, check the catheter tubing for kinks to relieve bladder distension, and loosen tight clothing. • Neurogenic Bowel and Bladder: Voluntary sphincter control is lost. Clients require scheduled intermittent catheterization or indwelling catheters, and strict daily bowel routines (suppositories, digital stimulation) to prevent impaction. • Extreme Risk of Pressure Ulcers: Complete loss of sensory pain feedback means clients cannot feel ischemic pressure pain on their sacrum, ischium, and heels. Strict repositioning every 2 hours in bed and weight shifts every 15 minutes in a wheelchair are mandatory.
脊髓损伤患者面临多项危及生命的慢性病理并发症，需要支持工作者保持最高级别的专业警觉： • 自主神经反射异常（Autonomic Dysreflexia）：发生在损伤平面位于第6胸髓（T6）及以上的脊髓损伤长者身上的神经科急性致命危象！当损伤平面以下的部位受到伤害性刺激时——最常见诱因包括膀胱极度膨胀过度充盈（留置导尿管受压扭曲堵塞）、严重直肠粪便嵌塞，或下肢被烫伤、压疮压迫。损伤下方的脊髓反射性暴发大量未受大脑调控的失控性交感神经兴奋冲动，导致全身内脏大血管剧烈收缩。症状：血压毫无预兆地发生爆炸式恶性骤升（收缩压常飙升至200至300 mmHg以上）、搏动性剧烈炸裂样头痛、损伤平面以上皮肤大汗淋漓潮红、损伤平面以下苍白冰凉伴起鸡皮疙瘩。该高血压危象可在数分钟内诱发大面积脑出血、致命性抽搐或心跳骤停！急救规程：立即协助长者坐起呈90度端坐位（利用重力使下肢血管床血液淤积以降低颅内灌注压）；立即呼叫主管护士；火速检查导尿管是否打折扭曲，立即排空膀胱解除尿潴留；解开紧身裤带与衣服。 • 神经源性肠道与膀胱：自主排尿排便反射中断。长者需定时行无菌间歇性导尿或终身留置导尿管，并严格推行每日定时结肠排便程序（开塞露灌肠、指压刺激直肠反射）以防粪石梗阻。 • 极度压疮易感性：深浅感觉及痛温觉完全丧失，患者无法感知坐骨结节、骶尾部及足跟部受压缺血的刺痛。必须严格执行卧床每2小时轴线翻身、坐轮椅每15分钟施行一次臀部悬空减压（Push-up减压），否则深部压疮可深达骨膜诱发致死性脓毒血症。
PAGE 787 · 第 787 页 Figure 33.15 (SCI Paralysis) & Parkinson's Disease / 图 33.15（脊髓瘫痪平面图谱）与帕金森病病理与照护

SPINAL CORD INJURY LEVELS & PARKINSON'S DISEASE

脊髓神经麻痹受损区域图解与帕金森病中枢变性

FIGURE 33.15: Areas of paralysis resulting from spinal cord injury. High cervical lesions cause quadriplegia (shaded purple, affecting both arms, trunk, and legs), whereas thoracic and lumbar lesions cause paraplegia (shaded blue, affecting legs and lower trunk).

图 33.15：脊髓损伤引起的瘫痪区域分布图解。颈髓高位损伤引发累及双臂、躯干与双腿的四肢瘫（紫色阴影部分）；而胸腰髓节段损伤则引起累及下半身与双下肢的截瘫（蓝色阴影部分）。

PARKINSON'S DISEASE: PATHOLOGY AND CORE SYMPTOMS

帕金森病（震颤麻痹）：基底核病理机制与四大核心运动体征
Parkinson's disease is a chronic, progressive neurodegenerative movement disorder. It is caused by the gradual, unexplained degeneration and death of dopamine-producing neurons in the substantia nigra of the basal ganglia in the midbrain. Dopamine is the crucial inhibitory neurotransmitter that enables smooth, fluid, and coordinated muscle movements. When dopamine levels plummet by 60% to 80%, acetylcholine excitation runs unchecked, producing the classic cardinal tetrad of motor symptoms (TRAP): 1. T - Tremor: Resting tremor, usually starting unilaterally in one hand or fingers, resembling a rhythmic 'pill-rolling' motion (at 4 to 6 Hz). The tremor is prominent at rest and diminishes during purposeful voluntary movement or sleep. 2. R - Rigidity: Increased muscular resistance to passive joint movement, described as 'lead-pipe' rigidity (constant smooth resistance) or 'cogwheel' rigidity (jerky, ratchet-like resistance due to superimposed tremor). 3. A - Akinesia / Bradykinesia: Extreme slowness of voluntary movement and poverty of spontaneous movement. Manifests as a fixed, unblinking 'mask-like' facial expression (hypomimia), infrequent swallowing causing drooling (sialorrhea), soft monotone speech (hypophonia), and microscopic handwriting (micrographia). 4. P - Postural Instability: Loss of normal postural righting reflexes. The client stands in a stooped forward-flexed posture and exhibits a characteristic 'festinating gait'—shuffling with short, accelerating steps, with arms fixed and not swinging, unable to stop or turn safely, carrying an extreme risk for devastating falls.
帕金森病（Parkinson's Disease，PD）是一种以运动障碍为主要特征的慢性、进行性神经系统退行性变性疾病。其根本病理改变是中脑黑质纹状体系统多巴胺能神经元发生特异性进行性变性坏死与脱失，导致神经递质多巴胺（Dopamine）合成显著锐减。多巴胺是抑制肌肉过度兴奋、协调肌肉平滑随意运动的核心神经递质。当多巴胺神经元丧失超过60%至80%时，胆碱能神经系统兴奋性相对过剩，导致临床著名的四大经典运动核心体征（TRAP法则）： 1. T（Tremor 静止性震颤）：常从一侧上肢远端手指开始，呈特征性的「搓丸样」（Pill-rolling）节律性摆动震颤（频率约为4至6赫兹）。静止松弛状态下震颤最显著，情绪紧张激动时加剧，随意运动时减轻，入睡后完全消失。 2. R（Rigidity 肌肉强直）：伸肌和屈肌张力同时增高，被动活动其关节时阻力持续均匀增大，类似弯曲铅管（「铅管样强直」Lead-pipe）；若伴有震颤，被动屈伸关节时会产生断续齿轮样顿挫感（「齿轮样强直」Cogwheel rigidity）。 3. A（Akinesia / Bradykinesia 运动迟缓与动作贫乏）：随意运动启动极其困难、动作幅度明显减小。面部表情肌活动贫乏，双眼眨眼极少、表情呆板固定呈典型的「面具脸」（Mask-like face）；吞咽反射迟钝引起唾液咽下不及时而出现自发流涎；言语低沉单调含糊；写字越写越小（「小字征」Micrographia）。 4. P（Postural Instability 姿势步态不稳）：身体平衡反射调节障碍。患者站立呈头颈躯干前倾、双肘双膝微屈的特殊屈曲姿势；行走时迈步极其艰难（起步犹豫冻结），一旦迈开脚步则步幅极小、呈前冲擦地的小碎步向前狂奔，双臂缺乏正常的自发摆动，无法自如减速或转弯（「慌张步态」Festinating gait），具有极高的跌倒骨折风险。
PAGE 788 · 第 788 页 Huntington's Disease & Multiple Sclerosis (MS) / 亨廷顿舞蹈病遗传机制与多发性硬化症脱髓鞘改变

HUNTINGTON'S DISEASE (HUNTINGTON'S CHOREA)

亨廷顿舞蹈病：常染色体显性遗传神经系统退行性病变
Huntington’s disease is a rare, inherited, progressive, fatal neurodegenerative disorder caused by an autosomal dominant genetic mutation (CAG trinucleotide repeat expansion in the HTT gene). Every child of an affected parent has a 50% chance of inheriting the disease. Symptoms usually appear between ages 30 and 50, progressing relentlessly over 15 to 20 years to severe disability and death. Clinical Triad of Huntington's Disease: 1. Chorea: Involuntary, irregular, spasmodic, rapid, jerky, dancing-like movements of the face, limbs, and trunk. In early stages, movements resemble restlessness; in advanced stages, violent flailing impairs walking, speaking, swallowing, and breathing. 2. Cognitive Decline & Dementia: Progressive destruction of basal ganglia and cortical neurons causes severe memory loss, loss of reasoning, inability to organize thoughts, and eventual profound dementia. 3. Psychiatric Disturbances: Marked personality changes, severe clinical depression, explosive irritability, psychosis, and a very high risk of suicide. PSW Care: Provide a high-calorie diet (chorea burns thousands of calories daily); puree foods and thicken liquids for dysphagia; maintain safety to prevent injury from flailing limbs; ensure calm, non-judgmental emotional support.
亨廷顿舞蹈病（Huntington’s Disease）是一种罕见的常染色体显性遗传性进行性致命神经系统退行性疾病（HTT基因内CAG三核苷酸串联重复序列过度扩增）。患病父母的每一位子女均有高达50%的概率遗传该突变基因。临床症状通常在30至50岁中年期悄然发病，在随后的15至20年间进行性恶化直至全身衰竭死亡。 亨廷顿病三大临床核心表征： 1. 舞蹈样动作（Chorea）：面部、四肢及躯干出现不自主、快速、突发、粗大、无目的且无法控制的抽动与扭动，状如怪异步态的滑稽舞蹈。早期表现为坐立不安小动作；晚期不自主扑动极其剧烈，严重阻碍进食、言语、行走与正常呼吸。 2. 认知退化与痴呆：基底核纹状体及皮层神经元的大量凋亡坏死，导致近远期记忆丧失、逻辑推理瓦解、注意力彻底涣散，最终无可避免地演变为重度全面性痴呆。 3. 精神行为障碍：严重的人格骤变、极度狂躁激惹、严重抑郁、幻觉妄想以及极高的自杀倾向。 支持工作者照护要点：持续性不自主抽动每日额外消耗数千大卡体能，必须提供高热量、高营养密度的丰富膳食；针对重度吞咽困难提供糊状软食及增稠液体；在轮椅及床上加装软衬防护垫，防止剧烈扑动的四肢撞击坚硬物体骨折；给予充分的同理心安抚与尊严维护。

Multiple Sclerosis (MS): Central Demyelinating Disease

多发性硬化症（MS）：中枢神经系统自身免疫脱髓鞘
Multiple Sclerosis (MS) is a chronic autoimmune, inflammatory disease of the central nervous system (brain and spinal cord). In MS, the body's own immune system attacks and destroys the protective myelin sheath (the fatty insulating layer surrounding nerve axons), replacing it with hardened sclerotic scar tissue (plaques). When the myelin sheath is damaged, nerve impulses are slowed, distorted, or completely blocked from reaching target organs. MS is typically diagnosed in young adults between 20 and 40 years of age, affecting women twice as frequently as men. Canada has one of the highest prevalence rates in the world. Clinical Course and Symptoms: Most clients present with a Relapsing-Remitting course (episodes of acute neurological dysfunction followed by partial or complete recovery, though each relapse can leave residual deficits). Symptoms include extreme fatigue (overwhelming exhaustion triggered by minimal exertion), optic neuritis (blurred vision, eye pain), numbness/tingling, spastic muscle stiffness, tremors, ataxia, unsteady gait, bladder/bowel incontinence, and cognitive slowing. PSW Care Considerations: Heat sensitivity (Uhthoff's phenomenon—a small increase in body temperature temporarily worsens neurological symptoms) is critical. Keep the client cool, avoid hot showers or baths, schedule activities during cool morning hours, assist with energy pacing, and support mobility with assistive equipment.
多发性硬化症（Multiple Sclerosis，MS）是一种累及脑白质和脊髓的慢性自身免疫性炎性脱髓鞘疾病。在MS病程中，机体免疫系统错误攻击并摧毁包裹在神经轴突外表面的绝缘保护层——「髓鞘」（Myelin sheath），并在受损脱髓鞘区形成坚硬的纤维斑块硬化疤痕（Sclerotic plaques）。髓鞘遭破坏后，中枢神经电信号的跳跃式传导发生严重延缓、畸变甚至彻底传导中断。MS常在20至40岁青壮年期起病，女性患病率是男性的2至3倍；加拿大属于全球MS患病率最高的地区之一。 临床病程与典型体征：最常见的临床表型为「复发-缓解型」（Relapsing-Remitting——急性神经功能缺损发作，持续数周至数月后部分或完全缓解，但反复复发导致神经残损累积加重）。常见体征包括重度慢性疲劳（轻微活动即陷入极度衰竭）、视神经炎（视物模糊复视伴眼痛）、肢体麻木刺痛、骨骼肌痉挛性强直、意向性震颤、步态蹒跚摇晃、神经源性大小便失禁及认知反应迟钝。 支持工作者特殊照护红线：热敏感现象（Uhthoff's phenomenon）极其关键——患者微小的体温上升（即使仅升高0.5度）即可迅速导致神经传导阻滞恶化而使肢体无力瘫痪加剧！照护中务必保持居室环境凉爽通风，严禁给患者洗烫热水澡或长时间温热泡浴；主要ADL活动安排在清晨体温最低、精力最好的时段进行；严格执行体能能量节约与步调控制，指导安全使用轮椅与助行器。
PAGE 789 · 第 789 页 Amyotrophic Lateral Sclerosis (ALS) & Diabetes Mellitus Intro / 肌萎缩侧索硬化症（渐冻症）与糖尿病病理生理分型

AMYOTROPHIC LATERAL SCLEROSIS (ALS / LOU GEHRIG'S DISEASE)

肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS / 渐冻症）：下运动神经元变性
Amyotrophic Lateral Sclerosis (ALS), commonly known in North America as Lou Gehrig’s disease, is a rapidly progressive, uniformly fatal neurodegenerative disease that attacks upper and lower motor neurons in the cerebral cortex, brainstem, and spinal cord. As motor neurons wither and die, the brain loses the ability to initiate and control voluntary muscle movement. The denervated voluntary skeletal muscles progressively weaken, undergo severe atrophy (wasting), and develop visible fasciculations (muscle twitching). The Heartbreaking Locked-In Paradox: Crucially, ALS selectively destroys MOTOR neurons only! Sensory pathways remain entirely intact (the client feels touch, hot, cold, and pain normally), and autonomic control, bowel/bladder sphincters, hearing, vision, eye movements, and cognitive intelligence remain fully functional. In late-stage disease, the client becomes completely paralyzed, immobile, and nonverbal, unable to breathe without mechanical ventilation—yet remains trapped in a fully alert, intellectually sharp mind. Support workers must communicate with utmost dignity, never speak about the client as if they cannot hear, use eye-gaze computer communication devices, maintain aggressive skin integrity protocols, and support advanced care planning.
肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS），在北美通称卢·格里克病（Lou Gehrig’s disease），在国内通称「渐冻症」，是一种迅速进展、致死性的运动神经元退行性病变。它选择性侵犯大脑皮层锥体细胞、脑干运动神经核以及脊髓前角运动神经元。随着上下运动神经元进行性变性死亡，大脑彻底丧失了启动并传导随意肌运动指令的通路。失去神经支配的骨骼肌发生进行性无力瘫痪、极度萎缩消瘦并伴随密集的皮下肌肉震颤（肌束震颤 Fasciculations）。 残酷的「闭锁状态」临床悖论：最为关键的是：ALS仅仅选择性破坏「运动神经元」！患者的感觉神经通路完全完好无损（痛觉、温觉、轻触觉完全正常），自主神经、肛门膀胱括约肌、听觉、视觉、眼球转动肌肉以及大脑的认知智力、思维记忆完全保持最高水平的清晰！在疾病终末期，患者全身四肢与躯干骨骼肌完全瘫痪、无法发声说话、丧失自主呼吸能力必须依赖呼吸机维持——然而，患者拥有一个意识完全清醒、智力无比清晰却被完全囚禁在无法动弹的躯壳中的大脑（闭锁状态）。支持工作者必须以最高的做人尊严对待患者；绝不可把患者当作听不懂或意识不清对待而在其床旁随意议论；借助先进的眼控打字沟通系统耐受交流；执行最严密的防压疮良肢位摆放并协助安宁疗护支持。

ENDOCRINE DISORDERS: DIABETES MELLITUS (TYPE 1, TYPE 2 & GESTATIONAL)

内分泌系统疾患：糖尿病（1型、2型与妊娠期糖尿病）病理
Diabetes mellitus is a chronic metabolic disorder of glucose homeostasis characterized by persistent hyperglycemia (high blood glucose). The hormone insulin is produced and secreted by the beta cells in the islets of Langerhans of the pancreas. Insulin acts as a molecular 'key' that unlocks cell surface receptors, allowing glucose to move from the bloodstream into cells for energy production, or to be stored in the liver as glycogen. In diabetes, the body either produces no insulin, produces insufficient insulin, or cannot effectively utilize the insulin it produces. Three Major Types of Diabetes Mellitus: 1. Type 1 Diabetes (Insulin-Dependent Diabetes): An autoimmune disease where the body's immune system destroys pancreatic beta cells. Usually diagnosed in children, adolescents, or young adults. There is absolute insulin deficiency; clients must take daily subcutaneous insulin injections or use an insulin pump for life to survive. 2. Type 2 Diabetes (Non-Insulin-Dependent): Accounts for over 90% of all diabetic cases, typically developing in adults over age 40, though increasingly seen in youth due to obesity. The pancreas produces insulin, but the body's tissues are resistant to its effects (insulin resistance), and insulin secretion becomes progressively inadequate. Managed through healthy diet, exercise, weight loss, oral antidiabetic medications (e.g., metformin), and often supplemental insulin later in life. 3. Gestational Diabetes: Develops in pregnant women during the second or third trimester due to pregnancy hormones blocking insulin. It typically resolves after delivery, but the mother and child carry a significantly higher lifetime risk of developing chronic Type 2 diabetes later in life.
糖尿病（Diabetes Mellitus）是一种以慢性高血糖为特征的内分泌代谢紊乱性疾病。胰岛素（Insulin）是由胰腺胰岛内部的贝塔（β）细胞合成分泌的合成代谢激素。在正常生理状态下，胰岛素扮演着打开靶细胞膜受体的「分子钥匙」角色，促使血液中的葡萄糖被摄入骨骼肌、脂肪细胞和脑细胞进行氧化供能，或在肝脏转化为糖原储存。在糖尿病状态下，机体胰岛素绝对缺乏、相对不足或靶组织对胰岛素产生抵抗，导致葡萄糖无法被利用而滞留血液并从尿液溢出。 糖尿病的三大核心临床分型： 1. 1型糖尿病（胰岛素依赖型）：自身免疫系统错误攻击并彻底摧毁胰腺β细胞。多在儿童、青少年及年轻人群中突发发病。患者体内几乎完全无法合成分泌自身胰岛素（绝对缺乏），必须终身依赖外源性胰岛素每日多次皮下注射或持续胰岛素泵输注以维持生命。 2. 2型糖尿病（非胰岛素依赖型）：占临床全部糖尿病患者的90%以上，多在40岁以上成年及老年人群中缓慢隐匿起病，但因现代肥胖率上升在青少年中亦日益普遍。患者胰腺仍能分泌一定量胰岛素，但外周骨骼肌及肝脏组织对胰岛素发生明显抵抗（胰岛素抵抗），且随着病程迁延β细胞功能逐渐衰竭。临床主要通过医学营养治疗、规律有氧运动、控制体重、口服降糖药物（如二甲双胍）以及晚期联合胰岛素进行阶梯综合控制。 3. 妊娠期糖尿病（Gestational Diabetes）：孕妇在妊娠中晚期由于胎盘激素拮抗胰岛素而首次出现的糖代谢异常。产后绝大多数产妇血糖可迅速自行恢复正常，但这类女性及其后代在未来的数十年内进展为永久性2型糖尿病的风险增加数倍。
PAGE 790 · 第 790 页 Diabetes Acute Crises: Hypoglycemia vs Hyperglycemia / 糖尿病急性代谢危象：低血糖昏迷与高血糖危象鉴别急救

ACUTE GLUCOSE EMERGENCIES: HYPOGLYCEMIA VS HYPERGLYCEMIA

糖尿病急性代谢急症：低血糖症与高血糖高渗/酮症危象鉴别
Managing diabetes involves maintaining blood glucose within a narrow therapeutic target range (typically 4.0 to 7.0 mmol/L fasting). Dramatic excursions in blood glucose lead to life-threatening acute emergencies that the support worker must immediately recognize and differentiate: • HYPOGLYCEMIA (Low Blood Sugar / Insulin Reaction / Insulin Shock): - Definition: Blood glucose dropping below 4.0 mmol/L. - Causes: Too much insulin or oral diabetes medication, skipping a meal or eating late, delayed digestion (vomiting), or unexpected strenuous physical exertion. - Speed of Onset: SUDDEN (minutes to an hour). Hypoglycemia is an acute medical emergency because brain cells lack alternative energy stores and die rapidly without glucose! - Signs & Symptoms: Cold, clammy, profusely sweating skin; trembling, shakiness, nervousness; hunger; dizziness; rapid pounding heartbeat (tachycardia); slurred speech; irritability or sudden confusion; numbness around lips; progressing to loss of consciousness, seizures, coma, and brain damage. - Emergency Rule of 15 Treatment: If conscious, immediately give 15 grams of fast-acting simple carbohydrates (1/2 cup [120 mL] of fruit juice or regular non-diet soda; 3 to 4 glucose tablets; 1 tablespoon of sugar dissolved in water). Wait 15 minutes and re-test blood glucose. If unconscious, NEVER force fluids into the mouth! Call 911 immediately and administer injectable glucagon if trained and ordered. • HYPERGLYCEMIA (High Blood Sugar / Diabetic Ketoacidosis / DKA): - Definition: Blood glucose rising above normal levels (often >15.0 to 20.0 mmol/L). - Causes: Missing an insulin dose, excessive dietary carbohydrate intake, acute infection, illness, severe stress, or lack of exercise. - Speed of Onset: SLOW and INSIDIOUS (develops gradually over days or weeks). - Signs & Symptoms: Warm, dry, flushed skin; intense thirst (polydipsia); frequent massive urination (polyuria); dry mouth and mucous membranes; sweet, fruity-smelling breath (acetone breath); deep, rapid, laboured breathing (Kussmaul breathing); nausea and abdominal pain; drowsy lethargy progressing to diabetic coma. - Action: Immediately notify the nurse or supervisor for blood glucose verification and insulin adjustment.
糖尿病临床管理的核心在于将毛细血管血糖精细控制在目标安全治疗窗内（通常空腹为4.0至7.0 mmol/L）。血糖发生剧烈异常波动会诱发两种截然相反但同样致命的代谢危象，支持工作者必须具备秒级鉴别能力： • 低血糖症（Hypoglycemia / 胰岛素反应 / 低血糖休克）： - 医学界定：毛细血管血糖低于 4.0 mmol/L。 - 常见诱因：胰岛素或促泌剂降糖药过量、延误进餐或漏餐、进食后恶心呕吐导致糖分未吸收、或未加餐即进行剧烈意外体力消耗。 - 发病速度：发作极骤（数分钟至半小时内突发！）。低血糖属于最高优先级的神经科急诊，因为中枢脑细胞完全没有糖原储备，在缺乏葡萄糖灌注下脑细胞在数十分钟内即发生不可逆脑坏死！ - 临床表现：全身冷汗淋漓、皮肤湿冷苍白；双手双腿剧烈震颤发抖、心慌心悸大汗；极度饥饿感；眩晕无力、说话含糊不清；烦躁易怒、精神混乱；口唇周围麻木；若未及时干预，迅速陷入抽搐昏迷直至死亡。 - 经典「15法则」急救处理：若长者神志清楚，立即口服给予15克速效单糖（半杯约120毫升纯果汁或普通非无糖碳酸饮料；3至4片医用葡萄糖咀嚼片；或一大勺溶于温水的白糖）；静坐等待15分钟后复测指尖血糖。若长者已意识不清或昏迷，绝对严禁强行向口中灌入液体以防窒息！立即拨打911并由护士皮下肌注胰高血糖素（Glucagon）。 • 高血糖症（Hyperglycemia / 糖尿病酮症酸中毒 DKA / 高渗高血糖综合征）： - 医学界定：指尖血糖异常升高（通常超过15.0至20.0 mmol/L以上）。 - 常见诱因：漏打胰岛素或自行停药、过量摄入高碳水饮食、合并急性严重肺部或尿路感染、急性心梗或严重创伤应激。 - 发病速度：起病极其隐匿缓慢（在数天乃至数周内逐渐加重累积）。 - 临床表现：皮肤干燥、滚烫潮红无汗；极度口渴烦渴多饮（Polydipsia）；大量排尿尿崩（Polyuria）；黏膜干燥眼窝凹陷；特征性的呼吸中呼出烂苹果样甜烂水果味（丙酮呼吸 Acetone breath）；呼吸深大而急促（Kussmaul库斯莫尔呼吸）；厌食恶心腹痛；精神极度萎靡嗜睡直至陷入糖尿病酮症昏迷。 - 处置：立即报告主管护士，配合测定血糖并遵医嘱静脉补充生理盐水与胰岛素纠偏。
PAGE 791 · 第 791 页 Diabetic Foot Care Protocols & Thyroid Gland Disorders / 糖尿病足保护规程与甲状腺疾病（甲亢 vs 甲减）

PREVENTING AMPUTATION: DIABETIC FOOT CARE PROTOCOLS

预防下肢截肢：糖尿病足部日常检查与护理实操红线
Diabetes damages both large and small blood vessels (peripheral artery disease causing ischemia) and destroys peripheral nerves (diabetic peripheral neuropathy causing complete loss of pain and temperature sensation). A client can step on a tack, walk with a pebble in their shoe, or develop severe friction blisters without feeling the slightest discomfort. If unnoticed, bacteria penetrate the ischemic wound, rapidly creating deep diabetic foot ulcers, osteomyelitis, and gangrene requiring amputation. Mandatory Support Worker Diabetic Foot Care Guidelines: 1. Daily Inspection: Inspect the client's feet every day, including the tops, soles, heels, and between all toes under good lighting. Look meticulously for redness, blisters, scratches, ingrown toenails, cracks, maceration, or skin breakdown. Use a hand mirror to inspect the soles if the client cannot lift their legs. 2. Gentle Washing: Wash feet daily with lukewarm water (always test water temperature with your inner wrist, never hotter than 37°C / 98.6°F) and mild soap. Never soak diabetic feet for prolonged periods, as soaking causes skin maceration and cracking. 3. Thorough Drying: Pat the feet dry gently with a soft towel; ensure the skin between the toes is completely dry to prevent fungal athlete's foot. 4. Moisturizing: Apply a physician-approved moisturizing lotion to the tops and soles of the feet to prevent dry cracking; NEVER apply lotion between the toes (trapped moisture fosters fungal and bacterial infection). 5. ABSOLUTE RED LINE: Never trim diabetic toenails! Support workers are strictly forbidden from cutting diabetic toenails, corns, or calluses. Trimming must be performed exclusively by an authorized chiropodist, podiatrist, or specialized foot-care nurse. Even a microscopic nick of the cuticle can lead to gangrene. 6. Footwear: Clients must wear clean, seamless cotton socks and properly fitted shoes at all times. NEVER allow a diabetic client to walk barefoot, even indoors on carpeted floors.
糖尿病对下肢的打击具有双重致命性：一是大中小动脉硬化闭塞导致下肢严重缺血，二是慢性高血糖毒性彻底破坏外周感觉神经（周围神经病变造成痛温觉完全丧失）。长者脚踩上图钉、鞋内有尖锐碎石磨破皮肤、或袜子勒破脚踝，其自身完全没有任何疼痛感知。微小伤口一旦破损，深部细菌迅速侵袭并在缺血软组织中蔓延，数周内恶化为深部糖尿病坏疽溃疡与骨髓炎，最终难逃截肢命运。 支持工作者必须坚决执行的糖尿病足部护理铁律： 1. 每日全方位检视：每日在良好光线下细致检视长者双足全貌，包括足背、足底、足跟以及「每一个脚趾间缝隙」。严密排查微小发红、摩擦水疱、划痕、倒刺嵌甲、皲裂脱皮或任何渗液。若长者无法抬腿，使用手持反光镜协助检查足底。 2. 温和清洗双足：每日用温水（必须用护理员手腕内侧试温，水温绝不可超过37摄氏度，防止烫伤）和温和香皂清洗双足。严禁长时间热水泡脚！过久浸泡会导致角质层软化发白浸渍（Maceration），极易皲裂破损。 3. 彻底吸干水分：用柔软毛巾轻柔蘸干，特别必须确保每一个脚趾缝间隙完全干燥，杜绝真菌足癣滋生。 4. 润肤乳涂抹规范：在足背和足底均匀涂抹医用润肤乳以防皮肤干燥开裂；严禁将润肤乳涂抹在脚趾缝隙之间！趾间积聚乳霜会造成局部密闭潮湿，为细菌真菌繁殖提供温床。 5. 绝对执业红线禁区：严禁支持工作者为糖尿病患者修剪脚趾甲！规程严格禁止支持工作者修剪糖尿病长者的趾甲、挑鸡眼或修刮胼胝（脚垫）。任何趾甲修剪工作必须由具有法定资质的足病医生（Podiatrist / Chiropodist）或专科伤口造口护士执行。即使用指甲刀剪破一毫米微小甲床皮下组织，都足以点燃坏疽截肢的灾难。 6. 保护性鞋袜：长者必须全天候穿着无接缝、透气的柔软纯棉白袜（便于及时发现渗血）以及合脚防滑的鞋子。严禁糖尿病长者赤脚行走——即便在室内地毯上也绝对禁止光脚走动！

THYROID DISORDERS: HYPERTHYROIDISM VS HYPOTHYROIDISM

甲状腺功能失调：甲状腺功能亢进症与减退症病理对比
The butterfly-shaped thyroid gland located in the anterior neck produces thyroid hormones (thyroxine / T4 and triiodothyronine / T3), which regulate the basal metabolic rate, oxygen consumption, and cellular heat production of every organ in the body: • Hyperthyroidism (Overactive Thyroid / Graves' Disease): - Pathology: The thyroid gland produces excessive amounts of thyroid hormones, dramatically accelerating cellular metabolism throughout the entire body. - Clinical Picture: Unintentional rapid weight loss despite an increased, voracious appetite; heat intolerance and profuse sweating; persistent tachycardia, palpitations, or atrial fibrillation; fine muscle tremors of hands; extreme nervous irritability and anxiety; insomnia; frequent bowel movements / diarrhea; and exophthalmos (protruding, bulging eyeballs due to retro-orbital autoimmune edema). • Hypothyroidism (Underactive Thyroid / Hashimoto's Thyroiditis): - Pathology: The thyroid produces insufficient amounts of thyroid hormones, severely slowing down all biochemical and physiological processes in the body. - Clinical Picture: Unexplained weight gain despite poor appetite; extreme cold intolerance (feeling frozen in warm rooms); bradycardia (slow heart rate <60 bpm); persistent chronic fatigue, profound lethargy, and excessive sleepiness; dry, coarse, scaly skin and brittle hair/nails; chronic severe constipation; slow monotone speech, memory slowing ('brain fog'); and non-pitting facial edema (myxedema). Treated with lifelong oral synthetic thyroid hormone replacement (levothyroxine), taken on an empty stomach.
位于颈前部气管前方的蝴蝶状甲状腺分泌甲状腺激素（甲状腺素T4与三碘甲状腺原氨酸T3），负责精确调控全身每一个细胞的基础代谢率、耗氧速度与体温能量生成： • 甲状腺功能亢进症（Hyperthyroidism / 甲亢 / Graves病）： - 病理机制：甲状腺腺体增生过度分泌大量甲状腺激素，导致机体各器官系统的新陈代谢被异常疯狂加速（高代谢综合征）。 - 典型表现：食欲大增多食却进行性暴瘦体重锐减；极度怕热、多汗皮肤潮湿；持续性窦性心动过速、心慌心悸或诱发房颤；双手细微震颤；情绪极度易激惹、焦虑多语失眠；肠蠕动亢进导致每日频繁腹泻；以及特征性的突眼征（Exophthalmos——双侧眼球向外突出、眼裂增宽呈凝视惊恐状）。 • 甲状腺功能减退症（Hypothyroidism / 甲减 / 桥本甲状腺炎）： - 病理机制：甲状腺激素合成分泌严重不足，导致机体全身基础代谢率全面深度减退迟缓（低代谢综合征）。 - 典型表现：食欲低下进食极少却体重持续顽固增加；极度畏寒怕冷（炎炎夏日仍需穿戴厚毛衣）；心率减慢（心动过缓<60次/分）；深度疲乏嗜睡无精打采、整日昏睡不起；皮肤干燥粗糙蜡黄如羊皮纸、毛发干枯稀疏脱落；肠麻痹导致顽固性便秘；说话言语缓慢低沉、反应呆滞记忆力衰退；以及面颊眼睑下肢非凹陷性水肿（黏液性水肿 Myxedema）。治疗需终身每日晨起空腹口服左甲状腺素钠片（Levothyroxine）进行替代替代治疗。
PAGE 792 · 第 792 页 Cardiovascular Disorders: Hypertension & Hypotension / 心血管系统疾病：高血压（无声杀手）与体位性低血压

CARDIOVASCULAR DISORDERS: HYPERTENSION (HIGH BLOOD PRESSURE)

心血管系统疾病：高血压（Hypertension）——隐形致命杀手
Hypertension is defined as persistent blood pressure measurements above the normal range—specifically a systolic blood pressure equal to or greater than 140 mm Hg, or a diastolic blood pressure equal to or greater than 90 mm Hg (or >130/80 mm Hg for clients with diabetes). Blood pressure is the lateral mechanical force exerted by blood against the arterial walls. Primary (essential) hypertension accounts for 90% to 95% of cases, linked to genetic predisposition, advancing age, high sodium intake, obesity, physical inactivity, smoking, and chronic stress. The 'Silent Killer' Paradox: Hypertension is called the 'silent killer' because it usually develops and persists for decades with ZERO signs or symptoms. Millions of people have dangerous hypertension without knowing it. Meanwhile, the unyielding mechanical high pressure silently causes vascular endothelial tearing, arterial wall hardening (arteriolosclerosis), and target organ destruction: • Heart: Heart must pump harder against high systemic resistance (afterload), causing left ventricular hypertrophy (LVH), coronary artery disease, heart attack, and congestive heart failure. • Brain: Weakened cerebral arteries rupture causing hemorrhagic stroke, or accelerate atherosclerotic thrombosis causing ischemic stroke. • Kidneys: Destroys glomeruli capillaries, leading to chronic kidney disease and end-stage renal failure. • Eyes: Damages delicate retinal microvessels (hypertensive retinopathy), leading to irreversible blindness. Management includes a low-sodium (DASH) diet, regular cardiovascular exercise, smoking cessation, limiting alcohol, stress management, and daily antihypertensive medications (ACE inhibitors, beta-blockers, calcium channel blockers, diuretics).
高血压（Hypertension）被界定为持续测得的体循环动脉血压异常升高——具体为收缩压持续等于或大于 140 mm Hg，或舒张压持续等于或大于 90 mm Hg（对于糖尿病合并患者，临床更严格收敛至 >130/80 mm Hg）。血压是指血液在血管内流动时对单位面积动脉血管壁产生的侧压力。原发性高血压占全部病例的90%至95%以上，与遗传多基因易感、血管弹性随增龄硬化退化、长期高钠饮食、肥胖、久坐缺乏运动、吸烟及交感神经慢性过度激活密切相关。 「无声杀手」临床悖论：高血压被称为现代医学最危险的「隐形无声杀手」，因为高血压在潜伏演进的数十年漫长病程中，绝大多数患者完全没有任何自主不适体征（无头晕、无头痛、无异样）！数以百万计的人在浑然不知中承受着致命高压。然而，血管腔内持续承受的剧烈异常机械冲击力，在无声无息中侵蚀撕裂动脉血管内皮细胞，诱发血管硬化变厚管腔变窄，并在全身靶器官造成毁灭性器质破坏： • 心脏受累：心脏克服极高的体循环外周阻力强行泵血（后负荷过重），导致左心室心肌代偿性肥厚衰竭、冠心病心绞痛、心肌梗死及充血性心衰竭。 • 脑血管受累：脑细小动脉变性硬化变脆破裂引发致命性脑出血，或加速斑块形成引发大面积缺血性脑梗死。 • 肾脏破坏：摧毁肾小球微细滤过毛细血管网，诱发高血压肾小动脉硬化、蛋白尿，最终演变为终末期慢性肾衰竭尿毒症。 • 视网膜眼底损害：破坏眼底视网膜微血管（高血压视网膜病变），导致眼底视网膜出血渗出水肿、不可逆失明。 治疗干预包括严格践行降压低钠限盐饮食（DASH饮食模式）、规律中等强度有氧运动、坚决戒烟、严控饮酒、减压并规范每日服用降压药物（ACEI/ARB、钙拮抗剂、利尿剂等）。

Hypotension and Postural (Orthostatic) Hypotension

低血压与体位性（直立性）低血压防跌倒对策
Hypotension is defined as a systolic blood pressure below 90 mm Hg or a diastolic pressure below 60 mm Hg. Causes include acute dehydration, hemorrhage, severe heart failure, sepsis, and side effects of antihypertensive medications. Postural (Orthostatic) Hypotension: An abnormal drop in blood pressure that occurs when a person moves suddenly from a lying or sitting position to an upright standing position (systolic drops >20 mm Hg or diastolic drops >10 mm Hg within 3 minutes of standing). When standing, gravity pulls approximately 500 to 1,000 mL of blood into the lower extremities; in healthy people, baroreceptors immediately trigger vasoconstriction and tachycardia to maintain cerebral perfusion. In older clients, this reflex is delayed, causing transient cerebral hypoperfusion. Symptoms: Dizziness, lightheadedness, blurred vision ('blacking out'), shakiness, syncope (fainting), and sudden falls. Support Worker Prevention Protocol: 1. The Dangle Rule: Never rush a client to stand! When getting a client out of bed, assist them to sit on the side of the bed with feet dangling for 1 to 2 minutes before standing. 2. Ask how they feel: Inquire if they feel dizzy. If dizzy, lie them back down immediately. 3. Gentle transitions: Encourage slow, deliberate positional changes, and ensure compression stockings (TED hose) are worn as prescribed to facilitate venous return.
低血压（Hypotension）通常被界定为收缩压低于 90 mm Hg，或舒张压低于 60 mm Hg。常见诱因包括严重脱水体液不足、急性出血失血、重度心力衰竭、严重感染性中毒性休克以及过量服用降压或利尿药物。 体位性（直立性）低血压（Orthostatic Hypotension）：是指当长者从平卧位或久坐位突然变换为站立位时发生的血压病理性剧烈跌落（站立3分钟内收缩压下降超过20 mmHg或舒张压下降超过10 mmHg）。当人体从卧位迅速站起时，受地球重力影响，约500至1000毫升血液会在数秒内瞬间淤积于腹腔与双下肢低位静脉丛中；健康年轻人的颈动脉窦压力感受器能瞬间反射性收缩外周血管并加快心率以维持大脑灌注。而在高龄长者中，这一神经调节代偿机制显著迟钝延缓，导致瞬时脑部严重缺血缺氧。 典型症状：眼前发黑（发蒙）、眩晕、站立不稳步态蹒跚、软弱无力、突发晕厥丧失意识跌倒骨折。 支持工作者防晕厥跌倒刚性操作规程： 1. 床边坐位「双腿悬垂法」（The Dangle Rule）：绝对严禁催促长者快速起身下床！协助长者下床时，必须分步操作：先协助其在床上缓慢坐起，双腿垂放于床边平坐1至2分钟（Dangling），给予充裕时间让循环系统血压平稳适应！ 2. 问询感受观察面色：坐定后主动询问其是否有头晕、眼前发黑或视物模糊。一旦长者诉头晕，必须立即协助其重新平卧床上！ 3. 动作极其缓和：鼓励缓慢变换体位；遵医嘱规范穿戴下肢弹力减压袜（TED hose），通过物理挤压促进下肢静脉血液逆重力回流心脏。
PAGE 793 · 第 793 页 Coronary Artery Disease (CAD) & Atherosclerosis / 冠状动脉粥样硬化性心脏病（CAD）病理机制与斑块演进

CORONARY ARTERY DISEASE (CAD) AND ATHEROSCLEROSIS

冠状动脉粥样硬化性心脏病（CAD）：冠脉循环与粥样硬化斑块
The heart muscle (myocardium) works continuously throughout life, contracting approximately 100,000 times each day. The heart cannot extract oxygen from the blood flowing through its internal chambers; instead, it receives its own vital arterial blood supply through the right and left coronary arteries, which branch off the base of the aorta and encircle the heart like a crown. Coronary Artery Disease (CAD) is a pathological condition in which these coronary arteries become progressively narrowed or obstructed, severely restricting blood flow, oxygen, and nutrients to the myocardial tissue (myocardial ischemia). Atherosclerosis (Plaque Formation): Atherosclerosis is the underlying pathological driver of CAD. It begins with damage to the arterial endothelium caused by hypertension, smoking, high blood glucose, or elevated LDL ('bad') cholesterol. In response, circulating fat, cholesterol, calcium, cellular waste, and inflammatory macrophages infiltrate beneath the vessel lining, accumulating over decades into hard, yellow fatty deposits called atherosclerotic plaques. As plaques enlarge, they protrude into the lumen, narrowing the artery and making the walls rigid and stiff (hardening of the arteries). This diminishes coronary blood reserve, setting the stage for angina and myocardial infarction.
心肌（Myocardium）是人体终身一刻不停高速运转的泵血发动机，每日节律性舒缩泵血达十万次之巨。然而，心肌细胞本身无法直接从心腔内部流经的大量血液中摄取氧气；心脏完全依赖由主动脉根部发出、如同一顶花冠般环绕覆盖在心肌表面的左、右「冠状动脉」（Coronary arteries）及其细小分支来供应高含氧动脉血。冠状动脉粥样硬化性心脏病（Coronary Artery Disease，CAD），简称冠心病，是指供给心肌自身营养的冠状动脉管壁发生慢性粥样硬化病变，导致管腔进行性狭窄或完全闭塞，使心肌严重缺血、缺氧或坏死的一组极其常见的心血管疾病。 动脉粥样硬化（Atherosclerosis）斑块演进机制：动脉粥样硬化是冠心病最核心的原发病理基础。长期高血压的血流物理剪切冲刷、吸烟毒素、长期高血糖以及血液中高水平低密度脂蛋白（LDL「坏胆固醇」）对冠脉血管内皮造成慢性微损伤。损伤部位引发局部炎症反应，大量低密度脂蛋白胆固醇沉着于内皮下并被巨噬细胞吞噬形成泡沫细胞，逐渐结合纤维结缔组织与钙盐沉积，在长达数十年中演变为黄白色的脂质坏死核心硬化斑块（Atherosclerotic plaques）。随着斑块体积不断膨胀增大并凸入血管腔内，冠状动脉管腔被机械性严重挤压变窄，血管壁丧失弹性变得僵硬变脆（动脉硬化），心肌灌注储备严重不足，为诱发心绞痛与急性心肌梗死埋下了致命导火索。

FIGURE 33.16: Progression of coronary atherosclerosis. A, Cross-section of a normal healthy artery with a wide, smooth, unhindered lumen. B, Artery severely narrowed by fatty atherosclerotic plaque deposits, drastically compromising myocardial blood flow.

图 33.16：冠状动脉粥样硬化病理演变截面对比。A：正常健康动脉管腔通畅、内皮光滑、血流通畅无阻。B：严重动脉粥样硬化管腔，大量黄白色脂质斑块显著突入管腔，使血流通路极度狭窄变小，严重阻断心肌供血。

PAGE 794 · 第 794 页 Angina Pectoris Radiation (Fig 33.17) & Acute Myocardial Infarction / 心绞痛疼痛放射规律（图33.17）与急性心肌梗死病理急救（图33.18）

ANGINA PECTORIS: REVERSIBLE MYOCARDIAL ISCHEMIA

心绞痛（Angina Pectoris）：可逆性心肌短暂缺血发作
Angina pectoris literally means 'strangling in the chest' (angina = pain/strangling, pectoris = chest). It occurs when myocardial oxygen demand exceeds coronary arterial supply, typically triggered by physical exertion, cold temperatures, heavy meals, or emotional stress. The ischemic heart muscle produces lactic acid, stimulating cardiac pain receptors. Pain Presentation and Radiation (Figure 33.17): The pain is described as a heavy squeezing, tightness, pressing, or burning sensation beneath the sternum (substernal chest pain), often mistaken for indigestion. Classic radiation patterns: The pain commonly radiates up to the left jaw, neck, throat, between the shoulder blades in the back, and down the inner aspect of the left arm to the fingers. The pain usually lasts 2 to 15 minutes and is characteristically relieved within minutes by stopping all activity, resting quietly, and administering sublingual nitroglycerin (which dilates coronary vessels). Medical Protocol: If the pain is NOT relieved after rest and the prescribed dose of nitroglycerin (or lasts longer than 15 minutes, or occurs at rest), it must be treated as an evolving ACUTE MYOCARDIAL INFARCTION—call 911 immediately!
心绞痛（Angina Pectoris）字面释义为「胸部紧缩压榨绞痛」（angina=绞痛/扼死感，pectoris=胸部）。当体力活动、剧烈情绪激动、寒冷刺激、饱餐或暴怒导致心肌耗氧量急剧激增，而狭窄的冠状动脉无法充分扩张代偿供血时，心肌无氧代谢累积大量乳酸等酸性代谢产物，强烈刺激心脏感觉神经末梢引发短暂胸痛。 胸痛特征与经典放射规律（图33.17）：长者常将心绞痛描述为胸骨后或心前区拳头样压榨感、窒息样重压紧缩感、烧灼感或窒息感，极易被误认为是胃部消化不良或烧心。高度特征性的疼痛放射路径：疼痛常从胸骨正中向外放射至左侧肩胛骨内侧、后背、下颌部、咽喉部，并极其典型地沿左上肢内侧向小指无名指放射。典型稳定型心绞痛发作通常持续2至15分钟，在停止一切活动、立刻平卧或半卧静止休息、并在舌下含服硝酸甘油片（强效扩张冠脉血管）后2至5分钟内迅速完全缓解。医学急救红线：若长者休息并含服硝酸甘油后胸痛持续超过15分钟依然毫无缓解，或在完全静息状态下发生剧烈胸痛，必须高度警惕已恶化为「急性心肌梗死」——必须立即拨打911急救电话并汇报主管护士！

FIGURE 33.17: Typical pain distribution of angina pectoris. A, Primary substernal chest discomfort. B and C, Common radiation pathways spreading to the jaw, neck, back, shoulders, and down the inner aspect of the left arm.

图 33.17：心绞痛典型疼痛与放射分布区域。A：原发胸骨后及心前区压榨痛。B和C：经典牵涉放射痛通路——向上放射至左侧下颌颈部，向后放射至肩胛间区，并沿左上臂及前臂内侧放射至左手小指无名指。

MYOCARDIAL INFARCTION (MI / HEART ATTACK)

急性心肌梗死（MI / 心脏病突发发作）：心肌坏死病理与救治
Myocardial Infarction (MI), commonly called a heart attack, occurs when an unstable atherosclerotic plaque in a coronary artery ruptures, triggering immediate massive thrombus (blood clot) formation that completely occludes the lumen. Blood flow to the downstream heart muscle stops abruptly. Deprived of oxygen, myocardial tissue begins to die (infarction / necrosis) within 20 minutes (Figure 33.18). Dead heart tissue cannot regenerate; it is permanently replaced by non-contractile fibrous scar tissue, severely impairing cardiac pump function and frequently causing fatal ventricular arrhythmias (ventricular fibrillation). Symptoms of Acute MI: • Crushing, agonizing, severe substernal chest pain described as 'an elephant sitting on my chest,' not relieved by rest or nitroglycerin. • Pain radiating to left shoulder, arm, neck, jaw, upper abdomen, or back. • Profuse cold diaphoresis (clammy drenching cold sweat), extreme pallor or cyanosis. • Severe shortness of breath (dyspnea), choking feeling, orthopnea. • Nausea, vomiting, lightheadedness, and an overwhelming feeling of impending doom. • Atypical Presentations: Older adults, women, and diabetics frequently experience 'silent' or atypical heart attacks without classic chest pain, presenting only with sudden profound shortness of breath, unexplained fainting, extreme weakness, indigestion, or sudden confusion. Emergency Action: Call 911 immediately! Have the client sit quietly in a comfortable semi-Fowler's position; loosen tight clothing; assist with physician-ordered emergency chewable aspirin (160 to 325 mg) and nitroglycerin if blood pressure allows; monitor vital signs; and be prepared to initiate CPR and deploy an Automated External Defibrillator (AED) if cardiac arrest occurs.
心肌梗死（Myocardial Infarction，MI），即民间通称的「心脏病突发」（Heart Attack），系冠状动脉粥样硬化不稳定斑块发生破裂出血，血小板剧烈聚集在破裂口表面诱发急性闭塞性血栓形成，导致冠脉远端血流瞬间完全中断。失去了氧气和养分灌注，受累心肌在20至30分钟后即发生不可逆的透壁性缺血性坏死（Infarction / Necrosis，图33.18）。坏死的心肌细胞不可再生，最终只能由完全没有收缩功能的纤维硬化疤痕组织所替代，不仅严重削弱心脏整体泵血功能诱发心衰，更极易诱发致死性恶性心律失常（心室颤动室颤）。 急性心肌梗死经典临床警示体征： • 无法忍受的压榨性、濒死感剧烈胸痛，常被描述为「大象踩在胸口上沉重无比」，休息或连续多次含服硝酸甘油完全无效！ • 疼痛剧烈放射至左侧肩背部、左臂、下颌颈部或上腹部。 • 大汗淋漓（面色惨白发青伴全身湿冷大汗湿透内衣）。 • 严重呼吸困难喘憋、气促、濒死窒息感。 • 恶心呕吐、极度头晕眼花、濒死恐惧感。 • 非典型与隐匿发作：高龄长者、女性及长期糖尿病神经病变患者常发生「无痛性心肌梗死」，完全没有剧烈胸痛，仅突发表现为极度呼吸困难、无法解释的突发虚脱晕厥、恶心上腹痛或突发意识模糊！ 支持工作者现场急救生命红线：立即拨打911呼叫救护车！协助长者就地取半卧位平稳休息，严禁长者随意走动或搬动患者；松解衣领皮带；在护士医嘱指导下协助嚼服阿司匹林肠溶片（160至325毫克以抗血小板聚集）及舌下含服硝酸甘油；严密监测生命体征；一旦患者突发意识丧失心跳呼吸骤停，立即就地实施心肺复苏术（CPR）并迅速取用自动体外除颤仪（AED）除颤救命！

FIGURE 33.18: Myocardial infarction (heart attack). Complete occlusion of a coronary artery stops blood flow, causing death (infarction) of downstream heart muscle tissue, permanently impairing pump performance.

图 33.18：急性心肌梗死病理损伤图解。冠状动脉主要分支被血栓急性完全闭塞，阻断该供血区血流，导致该分支支配的下游大面积心肌组织发生不可逆缺血性坏死（暗红缺血坏死区），永久性破坏心脏力学泵血能力。

PAGE 795 · 第 795 页 Congestive Heart Failure (CHF): Left/Right Failure & Fluid Rules / 充血性心力衰竭（CHF）：左右心衰病理、肺水肿与体液限制铁律

CONGESTIVE HEART FAILURE (CHF / HEART FAILURE)

充血性心力衰竭（CHF / 心功能不全）：血液淤积病理与分型
Congestive Heart Failure (CHF), or simply heart failure, is an abnormal clinical condition occurring when the heart muscle becomes damaged, weakened, or stiffened to the point where it cannot pump blood effectively to meet the metabolic oxygen demands of the body. Common etiologies include previous myocardial infarctions (scar tissue), chronic hypertension, coronary artery disease, cardiomyopathy, and diseased heart valves. Because the failing cardiac pump cannot empty its chambers forward, blood backs up behind the heart, causing an abnormal accumulation (congestion) of fluid in the tissues and organs. Two Forms of Heart Failure (Left vs Right): • Left-Sided Heart Failure: The left ventricle fails to pump oxygenated blood out into the systemic aorta. Blood backs up into the pulmonary veins, increasing pulmonary capillary pressure and forcing fluid into the alveoli and lung tissues (pulmonary edema). Classic Signs: Severe dyspnea (shortness of breath), orthopnea (inability to breathe while lying flat; requires 2 to 3 pillows or sitting in a recliner to sleep), paroxysmal nocturnal dyspnea (waking up gasping for air), persistent wet cough with frothy pink (blood-tinged) sputum, audible lung crackles (rales), and profound fatigue. • Right-Sided Heart Failure: The right ventricle fails to pump deoxygenated blood into the lungs.
