Blood backs up into the superior and inferior vena cavae, causing massive congestion in systemic venous circulation. Classic Signs: Dependent peripheral edema (severe pitting swelling in the feet, ankles, and pretibial shins), distended jugular neck veins (JVD), hepatomegaly (liver enlargement with right upper quadrant tenderness), and ascites (fluid accumulation in the peritoneal abdominal cavity causing rapid abdominal distension).
充血性心力衰竭（Congestive Heart Failure，CHF），简称心力衰竭或心衰，是指各种器质性心脏病导致心肌收缩力显著减弱、舒张顺应性严重受损，心脏作为动力泵无法将足够的心搏出量泵入体循环以满足各组织器官代谢需氧量的一组复杂的临床综合征。最常见的原发诱因包括既往大面积心肌梗死遗留大块纤维疤痕、长期慢性未控高血压、重度冠心病、扩张型心肌病以及严重心脏瓣膜关闭不全或狭窄。由于衰竭的心脏无法将心腔血液向前有效泵出，血液不可逆地在心脏后方上游静脉系统中发生严重倒流淤滞，导致全身器官和软组织发生大量异常液体蓄积水肿（充血 Congestion）。 心力衰竭的两大解剖病理表现（左心衰 vs 右心衰）： • 左心衰竭（肺淤血为核心特征）：左心室泵血无力，无法将动脉血泵入主动脉。血液逆流淤滞于肺静脉系统中，导致肺毛细血管静水压急剧飙升，大量血浆滤过渗入肺间质与肺泡腔内引发急性或慢性「肺水肿」（Pulmonary edema）。典型体征：进行性劳力性呼吸困难、极其显著的端坐呼吸（Orthopnea——平卧时回心血量剧增加重肺淤血导致极度憋喘，患者必须垫高2至3个软枕半卧甚至整夜端坐于靠背椅上才能勉强呼吸）、夜间阵发性呼吸困难（熟睡中被剧烈憋醒濒死喘息）、咳出大量粉红色泡沫样痰液、肺底部双侧闻及密集湿罗音（水泡音）以及全身重度乏力。 • 右心衰竭（体循环体静脉淤血为核心特征）：右心室泵血障碍，无法将体循环回流静脉血有效泵入肺动脉。血液向上、下腔静脉系统广泛倒流淤滞，引发全身系统性静脉高压。典型体征：重度对称性下垂部位凹陷性水肿（双侧足背、踝关节及小腿胫骨前出现指压凹陷性严重水肿）、颈外静脉显著怒张充盈（JVD）、淤血性肝肿大伴肝区持续胀痛，以及大量腹水（Ascites——腹腔内漏出大量液体导致腹部极度膨隆腹胀）。

Support Worker Management of CHF: Strict Fluid Rules & Daily Weights

支持工作者针对心衰患者的照护铁律：严格限水限钠与每日体重监测
Managing clients with CHF requires rigorous adherence to institutional protocols to prevent sudden, fatal pulmonary edema crises: 1. Strict Fluid Restriction: The care plan often restricts total fluid intake (e.g., 1,500 mL or 1,200 mL per 24 hours). The support worker must measure and record every single drop of fluid consumed—including water, tea, coffee, soup, ice cream, gelatin, and ice cubes (count ice cubes as half their volume in water). Communicate the fluid limit clearly to the client and family; never offer extra fluids! 2. Daily Morning Weight Monitoring (The Gold Standard): Fluid retention is detected by weight gain long before visible edema appears! Weigh the client every morning immediately after the first morning void, before breakfast, wearing the same type of clothing, using the exact same scale. Report to the nurse immediately if the client gains 1 kg (2.2 lbs) in a single day, or 2 kg (4.4 lbs) in a week—a rapid weight gain indicates acute fluid retention and impending pulmonary edema. 3. Low-Sodium Diet: Sodium attracts water, expanding blood volume and overburdening the failing heart. Ensure the client adheres to strict low-sodium meals; remove the salt shaker from the table. 4. High Fowler's Positioning: Elevate the head of the bed to 45 to 90 degrees (semi-Fowler's or high-Fowler's position) to allow the abdominal organs to drop downward, maximizing lung expansion and easing respiratory distress. 5. Monitor for Pulmonary Edema Emergency: If the client develops acute severe dyspnea, rapid breathing, cyanosis of lips and nail beds, extreme anxiety, and starts coughing up pink frothy sputum, they are experiencing flash pulmonary edema—call 911 / emergency team immediately!
照护充血性心力衰竭服务对象必须恪守以下高度严密的临床操作规程，严防突发恶性急性肺水肿危及生命： 1. 严格执行体液限制铁律（Fluid Restriction）：医嘱护理计划通常设定极其严苛的24小时液体入量上限（如全天严格限制在1500毫升甚至1200毫升以内）。支持工作者必须精确测量并如实记录患者口服摄入的「每一滴液体」——包括饮用水、果汁、浓茶、咖啡、菜汤、冰淇淋、果冻以及冰块（冰块化水按其体积的一半计算入量）。向长者及家属反复解释限水对保护心脏的极端重要性，绝对严禁擅自给长者倒水加饮！ 2. 每日清晨固定称量体重（临床监测金标准）：体液水钠潴留在体表显现出肉眼可见的凹陷性水肿前，早已表现为体重的隐匿性快速激增！每日清晨称重标准规程：在早晨排空小便后、进食早餐前、穿着相同重量的单衣、使用同一台经过校准的体重秤称量。核心报警红线：若长者在一天内体重突增 1公斤（2.2磅）以上，或在一周内体重突增 2公斤（4.4磅）以上，必须秒级向主管护士报告！体重的骤增100%是体内潴留了数千毫升游离体液的直接证据，预示着急性肺水肿即刻暴发！ 3. 严格低钠限盐饮食：钠离子具有极强的水合潴留效应，高盐会急剧扩增循环血容量从而将衰竭的心脏压垮。确保长者严格进食无添加食盐的清淡膳食，从餐桌上彻底拿走盐瓶。 4. 采取高半卧位（High Fowler's）：将床头摇高45至90度维持半坐卧位或端坐位，使腹腔内脏器官自然下垂，为膈肌向下运动提供最大空间，显著改善肺通气并减轻憋喘。 5. 急性肺水肿危象急救识别：若长者突发严重端坐呼吸、大汗淋漓面色青紫、烦躁惊恐并咳出特征性的「粉红色泡沫样痰液」，表明已陷入急性暴发性肺泡水肿——必须立即呼叫911急救团队并在高坐位下给予高流量吸氧救治！
PAGE 796 · 第 796 页 Cardiac Arrhythmias, Pacemakers & Phlebitis / DVT / 心律失常、心脏起搏器与静脉炎/深静脉血栓

Arrhythmias

心律失常（脉搏节律异常）
Arrhythmias are abnormal (a) heart rhythms (rhythmias). They happen when the heart's electrical system malfunctions. Sometimes, heartbeats are skipped, or extra beats occur. Usually, the client's health is not affected, but some arrhythmias are more serious. They can cause dizziness, shortness of breath, fainting, or death. Avoiding caffeine (coffee, tea, colas, and chocolate) and alcohol can sometimes prevent arrhythmias. Medications are needed for severe arrhythmias.
心律失常是指异常的心脏跳动节律（Arrhythmias 指无正常节律）。当心脏自身的电生理传导系统发生功能紊乱时即会引发此症。有时心脏会漏跳一次，或出现额外的早搏。在多数情况下轻度偶发的心律失常不影响健康，但某些严重的心律失常非常危险，会导致头晕、气短、晕厥乃至猝死。避免摄入咖啡因（咖啡、浓茶、可乐和巧克力）及酒精有时可预防发作；严重的心律失常则必须接受抗心律失常药物治疗。
Pacemakers—medical devices that are implanted in the client's body to regulate the heartbeat—are used to treat some arrhythmias. They monitor heart rates, and when necessary, they give small electric shocks to stimulate heartbeats. Pacemakers can run on batteries for several years. Clients with pacemakers should avoid any areas or equipment that has strong electrical or magnetic fields, such as some microwaves, security systems, or therapeutic magnetic mattress pads. People who have pacemakers should also ask their doctor about being around cell phones and MP3 players, high-tension wires, metal detectors, industrial welders, electrical generators, and even some machines for medical procedures, such as those used for magnetic resonance imaging (MRI).
心脏起搏器——植入服务对象体内用以调节心跳节律的精密医疗器械——常用于治疗某些严重心律失常。起搏器持续监测心率，必要时释放微弱的电脉冲刺激心肌收缩跳动。起搏器电池通常可持续工作数年。植入起搏器的服务对象必须避开存在强电场或强磁场的区域与设备，例如某些微波炉、安检门系统或磁疗床垫。此外，患者应就使用手机、高压输电线、金属探测器、工业电焊机、大型发电机以及诸如磁共振成像（MRI）等医疗检查仪器的电磁兼容安全向医生充分咨询。

Phlebitis and Venous Thrombosis

静脉炎与下肢静脉血栓形成
Phlebitis is inflammation of a vein. It occurs most often in the legs. Phlebitis can be caused by bacterial infection, chemical irritation, trauma, lupus, or a genetic condition that causes blood to clot more easily. Signs and symptoms of phlebitis include redness, warmth, swelling, and pain in the affected area.
静脉炎（Phlebitis）是指静脉管壁发生的炎症，最常见于双下肢。静脉炎可由细菌感染、化学性刺激、局部机械创伤、系统性红斑狼疮或导致血液高凝状态的遗传倾向引起。其典型临床体征与症状包括受累区域的发红、皮温升高、局部肿胀以及明显压痛。
A blood clot in a vein is called a thrombus. If a thrombus forms in a deep vein, usually in the leg, the condition is called deep vein thrombosis (DVT). An embolus is a blood clot that has broken loose and is floating in the bloodstream. If it travels to the lungs, it can block blood flow to the lungs, a condition called pulmonary embolism (PE), which is a life-threatening medical emergency.
静脉内形成的血凝块称为血栓（Thrombus）。若血栓形成于深部静脉（通常在下肢深静脉），该病症称为深静脉血栓形成（DVT）。脱落并随血流游走的血栓被称为栓子（Embolus）。栓子一旦游走至肺部并堵塞肺动脉血液循环，即形成肺栓塞（PE），这是一种可瞬间危及生命的紧急医疗危重症。

FOCUS ON OLDER PERSONS: Cardiovascular Changes / 老年人关注点：心血管系统生理性衰老与风险

  • As people age, heart muscle cells decline and heart walls may thicken. Arteries become less elastic and stiffer.
    随着年龄增长，心肌细胞数量逐渐减少且心壁生理性增厚，动脉管壁弹性减退、硬度增加。
  • Blood flow to vital organs decreases, and older adults may tire more easily. Position changes must be done slowly to prevent orthostatic hypotension and falls.
    重要脏器的供血量减少，老年人更容易疲劳；改变体位时必须缓慢进行，以防直立性低血压导致头晕跌倒。
  • Older adults may not experience the typical crushing chest pain during a heart attack; instead, they may show confusion, extreme tiredness, or shortness of breath.
    老年人在心肌梗死发作时可能缺乏典型的剧烈压榨样胸痛，常仅表现为意识模糊、极度乏力或单纯呼吸急促。
PAGE 797 · 第 797 页 Respiratory Disorders: COPD & Chronic Bronchitis / 呼吸系统疾病：慢阻肺、慢性支气管炎与哮喘

Respiratory System Disorders

呼吸系统常见疾患概述
The respiratory system is made up of the lungs and their airways. The airways bring oxygen into the lungs and remove carbon dioxide from the body. Respiratory disorders interfere with this function and can threaten life. When breathing is compromised, clients experience severe anxiety and the feeling of suffocation.
呼吸系统由肺脏及其呼吸道气管树构成。气道负责将氧气吸入肺泡并排出体内的二氧化碳。呼吸系统疾病直接干扰这一至关重要的气体交换功能，甚至严重威胁生命。当呼吸出现窘迫时，服务对象会产生强烈的窒息濒死感与极度焦虑。

Asthma

支气管哮喘：发作性呼吸困难与痉挛
Asthma is a respiratory disorder characterized by recurring episodes of paroxysmal dyspnea (intermittent spasms of shortness of breath); wheezing on expiration, inspiration, or both, caused by constriction (narrowing) of bronchi; coughing; and thick bronchial secretions of mucus. The client with asthma becomes short of breath and produces wheezing sounds, which may be frightening for them. The client may also experience a rapid pulse, perspiration, and cyanosis.
哮喘是一种以反复发作的阵发性呼吸困难（突发性呼吸痉挛性气短）为特征的呼吸系统疾病；因支气管收缩（狭窄）、黏膜充血分泌稠厚黏液，导致呼气相或吸气相出现明显的高调哮鸣音，伴随剧烈咳嗽。哮喘发作时服务对象极度气促并发出哮鸣声，心理非常恐惧，同时伴有心率过速、大汗淋漓及口唇甲床发绀（紫绀）。
Allergies, exercise, cold air, outdoor activity in hot, humid weather, smog, respiratory infections, and emotional stress are common causes of asthma attacks. Clients with asthma have medications to treat the disorder (fast-acting bronchodilator puffers and corticosteroid inhalers) but, for severe attacks, may require emergency department treatment. You must call emergency services as soon as you see your client in respiratory distress.
过敏原、剧烈运动、吸入冷空气、炎热潮湿天气下的户外活动、空气雾霾、呼吸道感染以及精神压力情绪激动均为常见诱发因素。哮喘患者通常备有控制与急救药物（速效支气管舒张吸入剂和吸入性糖皮质激素气雾剂），但重度哮喘持续状态必须送往急诊抢救。一旦发现服务对象出现严重呼吸窘迫，支持工作者必须立即启动紧急医疗救助程序。

Chronic Obstructive Pulmonary Disease (COPD)

慢性阻塞性肺疾病（COPD）
Chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) is a persistent lung disorder that obstructs the airways and makes breathing difficult. It is a progressive disease, gradually worsening over time. COPD includes two major disorders that often coexist: chronic bronchitis and emphysema.
慢性阻塞性肺疾病（COPD，简称慢阻肺）是一种以持续气流受限为特征的渐进性肺部疾患，阻塞呼吸道并导致呼吸困难。该疾病呈慢性进行性恶化。慢阻肺主要涵盖两类常常合并存在的病症：慢性支气管炎与肺气肿。
PAGE 798 · 第 798 页 Emphysema, Barrel Chest (Fig 33.19) & Oxygen Support / 肺气肿病理、桶状胸特征与低流量吸氧支持
Smoking is the most common cause of COPD. Long-term exposure to chemical fumes and to certain dusts is another cause. COPD cannot be cured but it can be controlled. The client must quit smoking and avoid secondhand smoke. Medications to open the airways are usually delivered by metered-dose inhalers (MDIs), or 'puffers'. Breathing exercises and oxygen therapy may also be ordered. Fluid intake is encouraged to decrease the thickness of secretions, and efforts are made to prevent respiratory tract infections.
吸烟是导致慢阻肺最主要的原发病因；长期暴露于工业化学烟尘与有害粉尘环境中也是重要致病因素。慢阻肺无法根治但可以通过综合干预加以控制。患者必须戒烟并严格避免吸入二手烟。扩张气道的支气管舒张剂通常使用定量吸入器（MDI，或称喷雾吸入器）给药；同时遵医嘱进行缩唇腹式呼吸锻炼及低流量氧疗。鼓励服务对象适量多饮水以稀释痰液易于咳出，并采取严密措施防范呼吸道感染。

Chronic Bronchitis

慢性支气管炎：黏液过度分泌与气道阻塞
Chronic bronchitis is a persistent inflammation of the bronchi. Large amounts of mucus are produced in the bronchi, and the bronchial walls swell. The mucus is thick and difficult to cough up, so it cannot be cleared completely and thus obstructs the airways, making airflow into and out of the lungs difficult. Therefore, the body cannot get normal amounts of oxygen. Coughing is the first and most common symptom of chronic bronchitis. The client also has difficulty breathing and tires easily. Eventually, breathing becomes difficult even when resting.
慢性支气管炎是气管支气管黏膜及其周围组织的长期持续性炎症。支气管壁充血水肿并分泌大量黏稠痰液，难以彻底咳出清除，从而堵塞小气道，阻碍气体进出肺泡。机体因此无法获得充足的氧气供应。慢性咳嗽与咳痰是其最初且最常见的临床表现；患者日常伴有进行性气促并极易乏力疲倦；随着病情进展，甚至在静息卧床状态下亦会出现呼吸困难。

Emphysema and Barrel Chest

肺气肿病理损伤与特征性桶状胸
Emphysema occurs when the walls of the alveoli (small air sacs) are damaged and lose their elasticity. As a result, the alveoli can no longer expand and contract properly. Air becomes trapped in the alveoli during exhalation and cannot be expelled completely. Over time, the trapped air causes the chest to enlarge, giving it a characteristic rounded, barrel-like appearance known as a 'barrel chest'.
肺气肿发生于终末细支气管远端的肺泡壁受损破坏并丧失弹性回缩力之时。受损的肺泡无法正常膨胀与回缩排气，呼气时大量残余气体被困于过度膨胀的肺泡腔内无法排出。随着病程发展，长期肺泡过度充气导致胸廓前后径明显增宽增大，呈现出特征性的膨隆圆形外观，在临床上被称为‘桶状胸’（Barrel Chest）。

Figure 33.19: Barrel chest caused by emphysema. Note the enlarged, rounded chest resulting from trapped air in damaged alveoli.

图 33.19：肺气肿引起的桶状胸特征。注意由于肺泡壁弹性破坏导致呼气残气滞留，胸廓前后径病理性增大呈圆桶状。

PAGE 799 · 第 799 页 Infectious Lung Diseases: Pneumonia, Flu & Tuberculosis / 肺部感染性疾患：重症肺炎、流感与肺结核

Pneumonia

肺炎：肺泡渗出与气体交换障碍
Pneumonia is an infection of lung tissue. Alveoli (tiny air sacs within the lungs) in the affected area fill with pus, mucus, and other liquid, and oxygen and carbon dioxide are not exchanged properly, causing insufficient oxygen in the blood. Pneumonia can be caused by bacteria, viruses, fungi, or aspiration of food, liquids, or vomit into the lungs. Symptoms include high fever, chills, productive cough with greenish or rust-coloured sputum, chest pain on breathing, rapid pulse, and shortness of breath.
肺炎是指肺实质实质组织的急性感染性炎症。受累区域的肺泡被脓液、炎性渗出黏液及液体充盈，氧气与二氧化碳无法进行正常交换，从而引发低氧血症。肺炎可由细菌、病毒、真菌引起，亦可因误吸食物、液体或呕吐物入呼吸道导致吸入性肺炎。临床症状包括突发高热、寒战、咳黄绿脓痰或特征性铁锈色痰、深呼吸时胸痛、心动过速及呼吸窘迫。

H1N1, COVID-19, and Subsequent Viral Variants

H1N1新型流感、COVID-19及后续突变株
Two known respiratory viral threats of global significance are the H1N1 influenza virus and COVID-19 (SARS-CoV-2). These are extremely contagious airborne illnesses that primarily affect the respiratory system. Germs spread through respiratory droplets when an infected person coughs, sneezes, or talks, and can also rest on hard surfaces such as countertops and doorknobs. When someone touches these contaminated surfaces and then touches their mouth, nose, or eyes, infection occurs. COVID-19 and subsequent variants (such as Omicron) have demonstrated high transmissibility.
具有全球公共卫生重大影响的两种急性呼吸道病毒感染是H1N1流感病毒和新型冠状病毒（COVID-19）。这些高传染性病原体主要攻击人体呼吸系统。病毒主要通过感染者咳嗽、打喷嚏或谈话时喷出的呼吸道飞沫在近距离传播，也可附着沉降在台面、门把手等坚硬物体表面。健康人员接触受污染物体后再触摸口、鼻或眼部黏膜即可造成接触传播。COVID-19及其后续突变株（如奥密克戎等）表现出极强的传播力与免疫逃逸特性。
The H1N1 virus emerged in 2009 in North America, affecting primarily younger populations with fever, cough, and severe respiratory symptoms. Although originally termed 'swine flu', it cannot be transmitted by consuming pork products. Canada's annual influenza season typically runs from late fall through spring (roughly October to April/May). Annual flu vaccination and updated COVID-19 boosters are critical preventive measures for healthcare support workers and vulnerable clients.
H1N1流感病毒于2009年春季在北美暴发，因人群缺乏免疫力而在年轻群体中引发广泛重症感染，主要表现为发热、剧烈干咳。尽管最初被称为‘猪流感’，但食用经过熟制的猪肉制品绝不会感染该病毒。加拿大的流感季通常从深秋持续至次年春季（大约为10月至次年4/5月）。每年定期接种流感疫苗及最新的COVID-19加强针是保障支持工作者及高危照料对象健康的关键屏障。
PAGE 800 · 第 800 页 Immune System: HIV Transmission, Progression & AIDS / 免疫系统疾病：HIV体液传播、病程进展与艾滋病

Tuberculosis (TB) Epidemiology and Global Impact

结核病流行病学与加拿大原住民高危现状
Tuberculosis (TB) remains a major global public health concern, particularly among individuals with compromised immune systems (such as those living with HIV or AIDS). While the overall rate of active TB in Canada has remained relatively stable (around 4.9 per 100,000 population), significant health inequities exist: the rate is disproportionately high among Canadian-born Indigenous peoples (21.5 per 100,000 population). Globally, approximately 10 million people fall ill with active TB each year.
结核病（TB）依然是严峻的全球公共卫生威胁，在免疫力低下人群（如HIV感染者或艾滋病患者）中尤为高发。尽管加拿大全国活动性结核病发病率相对平稳（约每10万人中有4.9例），但存在显著的健康不平等：加拿大本土出生的原住民发病率高达每10万人中21.5例，远高于全国均值。全球每年约有1000万人新发活动性结核病。

Supporting a Client Who Is Living With a Respiratory Disorder

呼吸系统疾患支持照护规范与感染防控
Clients with respiratory disorders experience severe anxiety when struggling to breathe. Support workers play a vital role in comfort and infection control: protect yourself and the client by wearing prescribed PPE (N95 respirators or surgical masks, gloves, face shields); follow Routine/Standard Practices at all times; change out of clinical uniforms before leaving work to avoid vector transmission; assist the client into high-Fowler's or orthopneic positioning (leaning forward over a bedside table supported with pillows); and space activities to avoid exhaustion.
呼吸系统疾病患者在发生呼吸困难时常伴有剧烈的惊恐不安。支持工作者在照护舒适与感染阻断中肩负重任：按医嘱和照护计划佩戴规定的PPE（如医用口罩/N95防护口罩、手套及面罩防护屏）；全流程严格执行标准预防；下班离开机构或照护现场前必须更换下工作制服，防止成为病原传播媒介；协助患者采取高半卧位（High-Fowler's）或端坐呼吸位（前倾伏于置有软枕的跨床小桌上以协助肋间肌辅助呼吸）；各项照护活动间隙合理安排休息，避免耗竭体能。

Immune System Disorders: HIV and Infection Biology

免疫系统疾患：人类免疫缺陷病毒（HIV）传播途径
The immune system protects the body from disease and infection. When the immune system is attacked, the body cannot defend itself against microbes. Human Immunodeficiency Virus (HIV) attacks and gradually destroys the immune system, specifically CD4+ T helper cells. HIV is transmitted exclusively through infected body fluids: blood, semen, pre-seminal fluid, rectal fluids, vaginal fluids, and breast milk. It cannot be transmitted through casual contact, saliva, sweat, tears, toilet seats, or insect bites.
免疫系统的核心职能在于保护机体抵御外来致病微生物的侵袭。当免疫系统自身遭受破坏时，机体便丧失了基本的抗感染防线。人类免疫缺陷病毒（HIV）专门侵袭并渐进性破坏人体的CD4+辅助性T淋巴细胞。HIV仅通过特定的受感染体液传播：血液、精液、射精前液、直肠分泌液、阴道分泌物及母乳。日常社交接触、唾液、汗液、泪水、共用马桶坐圈或蚊虫叮咬绝不会传播HIV。
PAGE 801 · 第 801 页 HIV/AIDS Clinical Care, Universal Precautions & Stigma / 艾滋病临床支持照护、全面防护与消除歧视

Clinical Stages and Symptoms of HIV Infection

HIV感染的临床分期与早期流感样症状
Some individuals develop mild flulike symptoms 2 to 4 weeks after being infected with HIV (the acute retroviral syndrome phase). During the early window period, antibodies may not yet be detectable by standard tests, though viral load is extremely high. Other people may remain asymptomatic for up to 10 years (clinical latency). Even when asymptomatic, infected individuals can transmit the virus. Common early symptoms include fever, fatigue, chills, sore throat, headache, muscle aches, joint pain, and swollen lymph glands.
部分感染者在感染HIV后2至4周内会出现类似轻度流感的早期急性期症状（急性逆转录病毒综合征）。在早期的‘窗口期’内，常规抗体筛查可能无法检出，但此时体液病毒载量极高且极具传染性。另有部分感染者可在长达10年的潜伏期内没有任何临床症状。无论是否有症状，HIV阳性者均能将病毒传播给他人。早期常见症状包括发热、极度疲劳、寒战、咽喉痛、头痛、肌肉酸痛、关节痛以及全身淋巴结肿大。

Acquired Immune Deficiency Syndrome (AIDS)

获得性免疫缺陷综合征（AIDS / 艾滋病）
Acquired Immune Deficiency Syndrome (AIDS) represents the advanced, life-threatening stage of HIV infection, typically developing when the immune system is severely damaged (CD4 count drops below 200 cells/mm3) or when specific opportunistic infections arise. Early initiation of combination antiretroviral therapy (ART) allows people living with HIV to suppress viral replication, preserve immunity, and achieve a near-normal lifespan. Without treatment, opportunistic infections such as Pneumocystis jirovecii pneumonia, Kaposi sarcoma skin tumours, severe wasting syndrome, and severe chronic diarrhea develop.
获得性免疫缺陷综合征（AIDS，即艾滋病）是HIV感染失控发展的终末危重阶段，通常在机体免疫功能遭遇严重摧毁（CD4淋巴细胞计数降至200个/mm3以下）或发生特定机会性感染时确诊。早期启动高效抗逆转录病毒联合治疗（ART，俗称鸡尾酒疗法）可极大抑制病毒复制，重建并维持免疫机能，使感染者享有接近常人的预期寿命。未经治疗的艾滋病期常并发卡氏肺孢子菌肺炎、卡波西肉瘤皮肤恶性肿瘤、极度消耗恶病质及顽固性慢性腹泻。

COMPASSIONATE CARE: Dignity and Stigma Reduction in HIV/AIDS / 全人关怀：维护艾滋病感染者尊严与消除社会污名化

  • Clients living with HIV/AIDS often experience devastating social stigma, isolation, and shame. Never judge or treat the client differently from any other individual.
    HIV/AIDS感染者常常承受巨大的社会偏见、歧视、孤独感与病耻感。支持工作者绝不可持有任何评判态度，必须给予一视同仁的尊重。
  • Practice Standard Precautions uniformly for all clients. Do not wear double gloves or unnecessary protective suits that isolate the client emotionally when routine care is delivered.
    全面贯彻‘标准预防’原则于所有服务对象。在进行常规非体液接触的生活照护时，切勿戴双层手套或穿戴夸张的防护服，避免给服务对象造成心理创伤。
  • Maintain strict confidentiality. Unauthorized disclosure of a client's HIV status is a severe violation of professional ethics and privacy legislation.
    严格坚守隐私保密底线。未经授权向无关人员泄露服务对象的HIV感染状况属于严重违背职业道德与侵犯隐私法律的违法行为。
PAGE 802 · 第 802 页 Autoimmune Diseases (Fig 33.20): Lupus, Scleroderma / 自身免疫性疾病：系统性红斑狼疮与硬皮病

Autoimmune Diseases Overview

自身免疫性疾病病理机制
Autoimmune disorders occur when the immune system mistakenly attacks the body's own healthy cells, tissues, or organs. Normally, antibodies defend against foreign pathogens; in autoimmune disease, autoantibodies damage host structures. Common autoimmune conditions include multiple sclerosis (MS), rheumatoid arthritis (RA), scleroderma, type 1 diabetes, celiac disease, inflammatory bowel disease (Crohn's disease, ulcerative colitis), Hashimoto's thyroiditis, Graves' disease, and systemic lupus erythematosus (SLE).
自身免疫性疾病发生于机体免疫系统发生识别障碍，错误地将自身的健康细胞、组织或器官当做外来抗原发起攻击之时。正常情况下抗体精准清除外来病原体；而在自身免疫病理状态下，自身抗体会无差别破坏自身靶器官结构。常见的自身免疫疾患涵盖：多发性硬化（MS）、类风湿关节炎（RA）、硬皮病、1型糖尿病、乳糜泻、炎症性肠病（克罗恩病与溃疡性结肠炎）、桥本甲状腺炎、格雷夫斯病以及系统性红斑狼疮（SLE）。

Figure 33.20: Body parts affected by autoimmune disease. Common target areas include the brain (MS), thyroid (Hashimoto/Graves), joints (rheumatoid arthritis), lungs, heart, liver, kidneys (lupus), digestive tract (celiac/Crohn), and skin (scleroderma/psoriasis).

图 33.20：自身免疫性疾病累及的全身器官分布。常见靶向器官包括大脑（多发性硬化）、甲状腺（桥本/格雷夫斯病）、关节（类风湿关节炎）、心肺、肝肾（系统性红斑狼疮）、消化道（乳糜泻/克罗恩病）及皮肤（硬皮病/银屑病）。

Systemic Lupus Erythematosus (Lupus / SLE)

系统性红斑狼疮：多器官损伤与特征性蝶形红斑
Systemic lupus erythematosus (SLE), commonly called lupus, is a chronic inflammatory autoimmune disease that can damage joints, skin, kidneys, heart, lungs, blood vessels, and the brain. A distinctive 'butterfly rash' across the bridge of the nose and cheeks is characteristic. Lupus is characterized by alternating flare-ups and periods of remission. Because symptoms vary widely, diagnosis can be complex. Support workers must follow the individualized care plan closely to manage fatigue, protect skin from sun exposure, and observe for joint pain or edema.
系统性红斑狼疮（SLE，通常简称为狼疮）是一种累及全身多系统的慢性炎性自身免疫病，可造成关节、皮肤、肾脏、心脏、肺部、血管及中枢神经系统的广泛损伤。鼻梁和双颊对称分布的特征性‘蝶形红斑’（Butterfly Rash）是其典型体征。狼疮病程呈现急性发作与临床缓解交替出现的特征。由于临床表现高度异质，确诊难度较大。支持工作者必须严格按照个体化护理方案协助管理疲劳、避免日光紫外线暴晒、并密切观察关节肿痛及水肿情况。
PAGE 803 · 第 803 页 Digestive Disorders: GERD, Vomiting, Cholecystitis / 消化系统疾病：胃食管反流、呕吐窒息防范与胆囊炎

Vomiting Care and Aspiration Prevention

呕吐照护规范与防止呕吐物误吸窒息
Vomiting (emesis) is the expelling of stomach contents through the mouth. Vomiting can lead to dehydration, electrolyte imbalance, and, most dangerously, aspiration—inhaling vomitus into the respiratory tract, which can cause choking and fatal lung damage. When caring for a client who is vomiting: position the client's head to the side or assist into a side-lying (recovery) position to prevent aspiration; provide an emesis basin; offer prompt mouth care (rinsing with fresh water or mouthwash) to remove corrosive gastric acid; measure, record, and report the amount, colour, odour, and presence of blood (bright red or 'coffee-ground'); and save vomitus for laboratory analysis if required by facility protocol.
呕吐（Emesis）是指胃内容物经口强行排出的反射性动作。呕吐可迅速导致脱水、电解质紊乱，以及最为危险的误吸（Aspiration）——胃内容物被吸入气道阻塞呼吸引起窒息或严重的吸入性肺炎。照护发生呕吐的服务对象时：立即协助其将头偏向一侧或调整为侧卧位（侧复原体位）以防误吸窒息；备好呕吐盆与纸巾；呕吐后立即协助漱口清洁口腔以去除强腐蚀性胃酸；准确测量、记录并向主管护士汇报呕吐物的量、性状、颜色、气味及是否含有鲜血或呈‘咖啡渣样’暗褐色；根据机构规范要求保留标本待检。

Gastro-Esophageal Reflux Disease (GERD)

胃食管反流病（GERD）：反酸烧心与床头抬高体位
Gastro-esophageal reflux disease (GERD) occurs when the lower esophageal sphincter relaxes inappropriately, allowing acidic gastric contents to back up into the esophagus. The most common symptom is 'heartburn' (a burning sensation in the chest behind the sternum), along with sour taste, bad breath, belching, and painful swallowing (dysphagia). Management includes antacid medications, eating small frequent meals, avoiding eating within 3 hours of bedtime, avoiding spicy/acidic foods, and elevating the head of the bed (HOB) by 15–20 cm with bed blocks or an adjustable frame to keep stomach contents down by gravity.
胃食管反流病（GERD）发生于食管下端括约肌松弛异常关闭不全，导致胃酸及胃内容物逆流入食管。最典型的临床主诉为‘烧心’（胸骨后灼烧感，Heartburn），并伴有口腔酸臭味、反酸嗳气及吞咽隐痛。护理管理要点包括遵医嘱服用抑酸药；少食多餐；避免睡前3小时内进食；避免高脂油腻、辛辣及酸性食物；利用床头垫块或升降床架将床头抬高15至20厘米，利用重力生理作用防止胃液夜间反流。

Gallbladder Disease and Cholecystitis

胆囊疾病与胆囊炎（胆石症引发绞痛）
The gallbladder is a small pear-shaped organ under the liver that stores bile. When components of bile crystallize, gallstones form, which can obstruct the bile ducts and cause acute inflammation called cholecystitis. Symptoms include severe right upper quadrant abdominal pain that may radiate to the right shoulder or back, nausea, vomiting, fever, and jaundice (yellowing of skin and eyes) if bile flow is blocked. Management includes low-fat diets, pain management, and surgical removal (cholecystectomy).
胆囊是位于肝脏下方的梨形肌性囊袋，负责浓缩与储存胆汁。当胆汁成分比例失调析出结晶时，便会形成胆结石并阻塞胆管，诱发急性胆囊炎（Cholecystitis）。其特征性表现为右上腹剧烈阵发性绞痛并常向右肩背部放射，伴恶心、呕吐、发热；若胆总管发生完全梗阻则会出现全身皮肤巩膜黄染（黄疸）。治疗手段涵盖严格低脂清淡饮食、药物镇痛及腹腔镜胆囊切除术。
PAGE 804 · 第 804 页 Irritable Bowel Syndrome, Crohn's Disease & Colitis / 肠易激综合征、克罗恩病与溃疡性结肠炎

Celiac Disease and Gluten Enteropathy

乳糜泻：自身免疫性谷蛋白不耐受与肠黏膜绒毛萎缩
Celiac disease is an autoimmune disorder where eating gluten—a protein found in wheat, barley, and rye—triggers an immune response that damages the lining of the small intestine. Specifically, the villi (tiny finger-like projections that absorb nutrients) are flattened and destroyed, leading to nutrient malabsorption, chronic diarrhea, abdominal bloating, weight loss, and fatigue. The only effective treatment is a lifelong strict gluten-free diet.
乳糜泻是一种自身免疫性吸收不良综合征，患者在摄入含麸质（存在于小麦、大麦、黑麦中的蛋白质）食物后触发异常免疫应答，严重破坏小肠内壁黏膜。特别是负责吸收微量营养素的小肠绒毛发生水肿萎缩与平滑化，导致严重吸收障碍、慢性水样腹泻、腹部胀痛膨隆、消瘦及极度虚弱。目前唯一有效的临床干预方案是终生严格遵行无麸质饮食（Gluten-Free Diet）。

Irritable Bowel Syndrome (IBS)

肠易激综合征（IBS）：功能性肠动力紊乱与共情支持
Irritable bowel syndrome (IBS) is a common gastrointestinal disorder affecting the large intestine, characterized by recurrent cramping, abdominal pain, bloating, gas, and alternating bouts of diarrhea and constipation. While IBS does not cause permanent damage to bowel tissue or increase the risk of colorectal cancer, it causes significant physical distress and emotional embarrassment. Support workers should maintain dietary logs to identify trigger foods, encourage adequate hydration and fibre intake, and provide compassionate, non-judgmental support during toilet urgency.
肠易激综合征（IBS）是一种常见的功能性肠道疾病，主要累及大肠，以反复发作的腹部绞痛、胀气、腹鸣以及腹泻与便秘交替出现为主要特征。尽管IBS不会引起器质性肠道组织坏死溃烂，亦不增加结直肠癌风险，但其引发的频繁急迫排便给服务对象造成巨大的心理负担与社交窘迫。支持工作者应协助记录饮食日记筛查食物诱因、保证充足水分与纤维摄入，并在患者出现如厕急迫时给予极具同理心与尊重的支持。

Inflammatory Bowel Disease: Colitis and Crohn's Disease

炎症性肠病：溃疡性结肠炎与克罗恩病
Colitis is inflammation of the colon characterized by abdominal tenderness, bloody diarrhea, rectal bleeding, and ulceration of the colon mucosa. Crohn's disease is a persistent inflammatory condition of the GI tract that can affect any segment from the mouth to the anus, but most commonly involves the terminal ileum and colon. Crohn's causes deep transmural inflammation leading to bowel wall thickening, strictures, fistulas, severe cramping pain, and frequent bloody stools (up to 20 movements per day). Intestinal obstruction can become life-threatening. Management requires anti-inflammatory medications, biologics, corticosteroids, and potentially surgical resection.
结肠炎（Colitis）是大肠黏膜的广泛炎性病变，表现为腹部压痛、黏液脓血便、直肠出血及黏膜表面溃疡形成。克罗恩病（Crohn's Disease）则是一种贯穿消化管全层的慢性炎性肉芽肿性疾病，病变可累及从口腔至肛门的消化道任何部位，以回肠末端及相邻结肠最常见。克罗恩病累及全层肠壁导致管壁增厚、肠腔狭窄梗阻、内瘘形成，引发剧烈绞痛与每日高达20多次的血性腹泻。严重的肠梗阻可危及生命。临床需采用抗炎药、生物制剂、糖皮质激素甚至外科手术肠段切除进行综合治疗。
PAGE 805 · 第 805 页 Diverticular Disease (Fig 33.21) & Urinary Tract Infections / 结肠憩室病、泌尿系统疾病与尿路感染

Diverticular Disease of the Colon

结肠憩室病：憩室形成（无症状）与急性憩室炎
Many people develop small, pouch-like sacs that bulge outward through weak spots in the wall of the colon. The presence of these small pouches is called diverticulosis, which is common in older adults and often produces no symptoms. However, when feces become trapped in these pouches, bacteria multiply and cause inflammation and infection, a painful condition known as diverticulitis. Symptoms include severe lower-left abdominal pain, fever, nausea, vomiting, and altered bowel habits. Complications include pouch rupture, abscess, and peritonitis.
随着年龄增长，许多人的大肠肌层薄弱处会向外膨出形成细小的囊状袋状物。这种囊袋的存在被称为憩室病（Diverticulosis），多见于老年人群且通常没有明显临床不适。然而，当硬粪块滞留卡在囊袋内时，细菌大量繁殖便会引发急性炎症感染，该病症称为憩室炎（Diverticulitis）。其特征为左下腹剧烈持续性疼痛、高热、恶心呕吐及排便异常。囊袋破裂穿孔可造成脓肿乃至致死性急性弥漫性腹膜炎。

Figure 33.21: Diverticular disease of the colon. Note the outward pouch-like herniations through the muscular layer of the large intestine wall.

图 33.21：结肠憩室病示意图。显示大肠管壁肌层缺损处向外突出形成的多个囊状憩室腔。

Urinary System Disorders: Urinary Tract Infections (UTIs)

泌尿系统疾患：尿路感染（膀胱炎）与诱因
The urinary system filters wastes from the blood and eliminates them in urine. Urinary tract infections (UTIs) are common and usually occur when microbes enter the urethra and travel upward into the bladder. Cystitis is an infection of the bladder. Women are at higher risk because of a shorter urethra located closer to the anus. Common symptoms of cystitis include urinary frequency, urgency, dysuria (painful or burning urination), foul-smelling urine, pyuria (pus/cloudy urine), hematuria (blood in urine), and low-grade fever. In older adults, sudden onset of confusion or altered mental status is frequently the primary indicator of a UTI.
泌尿系统负责从循环血液中滤出代谢废物并通过尿液排出体外。尿路感染（UTI）极其常见，主要由细菌经尿道逆行侵入并在膀胱内定植引起。膀胱感染称为膀胱炎（Cystitis）。女性因尿道解剖较短且距离肛门较近，感染风险显著高于男性。典型临床表现包括尿频、尿急、排尿灼痛（排尿困难）、尿液异味恶臭、脓尿（浑浊尿液）、血尿及低热。在老年服务对象中，突发的意识模糊或精神状态急剧改变往往是尿路感染最前驱、甚至是唯一的临床线索。
PAGE 806 · 第 806 页 Pyelonephritis, Kidney Stones & Acute/Chronic Renal Failure / 肾盂肾炎、肾结石滤尿观察与急慢性肾衰竭

Pyelonephritis (Kidney Infection)

肾盂肾炎：上尿路重症感染与肾实质损伤
If a bladder infection is not treated promptly, bacteria can ascend the ureters and infect the kidneys, causing pyelonephritis. Pyelonephritis is a severe infection causing high fever, shaking chills, severe flank/back pain (costovertebral tenderness), nausea, vomiting, and urinary urgency and burning. Treatment requires aggressive antibiotics and increased fluid intake. If left untreated, permanent kidney damage and sepsis can occur.
若下尿路膀胱感染未能得到及时有效的控制，致病菌可沿输尿管逆行上行侵入肾盂与肾实质，引发急性肾盂肾炎（Pyelonephritis）。这是一种严重的上尿路感染，伴有突发高热、寒战、剧烈腰背侧肋脊角压痛、恶心呕吐以及尿频尿痛。临床治疗需要足量广谱抗生素与大量饮水补液。若治疗不彻底，可造成不可逆的慢性肾功能损害甚至引发致命性脓毒症。

Renal Calculi (Kidney Stones) and Urine Straining

肾结石（肾结石绞痛）与滤尿筛查规范
Renal calculi (kidney stones) are hard mineral and salt deposits that form inside the kidneys. When a stone moves into the ureter, it obstructs urine flow and causes intense, excruciating renal colic—spasmodic pain radiating from the back to the lower abdomen and groin. Clients experience hematuria, nausea, vomiting, and chills. Support workers must strain all urine through a fine-mesh filter or gauze: any captured stones must be saved and sent to the laboratory for mineral composition analysis.
肾结石（Renal Calculi）是由尿液中的矿物质盐类过饱和析出形成的坚硬晶体沉积。当结石脱落移行进入狭窄的输尿管时，会阻塞尿流引发剧烈难忍的肾绞痛——疼痛呈阵发性刀割样，由腰部向腹下部及腹股沟区放射。患者常伴有肉眼血尿、恶心剧吐及冷汗。支持工作者在照护排石患者时必须执行严格的滤尿规程：使用专用的微孔滤网或纱布过滤所有排出的尿液，将截获的微小结石完整保存并送交实验室进行成分生化分析。

BOX 33.10: Straining Urine for Renal Calculi / 专栏 33.10：肾结石服务对象滤尿操作规程

  • Have the client void into a clean bedpan, urinal, or commode container. Never throw out urine before checking for stones.
    指导服务对象将全部尿液排入洁净便盆、尿壶或移动坐便器内。在未进行滤网检查前绝对严禁倾倒废弃尿液。
  • Pour the urine carefully through a fine-mesh urine strainer or gauze into the toilet bowl.
    平稳缓慢地将全部尿液经由专用微孔滤网或无菌纱布倒入马桶，严防溢洒。
  • Inspect the strainer carefully for any gravel, crystals, or small stone fragments.
    在充足光线下仔细检视滤网表面，搜寻任何微小沙粒、晶体或结石碎片。
  • If a stone is found, place it into a labelled specimen container and notify your supervisor immediately.
    一旦发现结石排出，将其小心装入贴有规范标签的无菌标本瓶中，并立即向主管护士汇报。

Acute and Chronic Renal Failure

急慢性肾功能衰竭（少尿期与尿毒症）
Renal failure occurs when the kidneys are unable to filter waste products from the blood. Acute renal failure occurs suddenly due to severe blood loss, shock, myocardial infarction, CHF, severe burns, or nephrotoxic toxins. It progresses through phases: first, an oliguria phase where urine output drops below 400 mL in 24 hours, causing fluid retention, edema, and hypertension; followed by a diuresis phase. Acute renal failure is often reversible with prompt intervention. In contrast, chronic renal failure (end-stage renal disease, ESRD) is a gradual, permanent loss of nephron function, most commonly caused by diabetes and hypertension.
肾功能衰竭是指肾脏完全或部分丧失滤过排除体内毒素与多余水分的病理状态。急性肾衰竭常由大出血、创伤性休克、重度心肌梗死、严重充血性心衰、大面积烧伤或肾毒性药物引起肾灌注骤降所致。其发展经历少尿期（24小时尿量少于400毫升），体内出现严重水钠潴留、高血压及肺水肿，随之进入多尿期；在严密抢救下常可逆转。而慢性肾衰竭（终末期肾病，ESRD）则是进行性、不可逆的肾单位永久性破坏，糖尿病肾病与高血压肾小动脉硬化是最主要的两大致病原因。
PAGE 807 · 第 807 页 Renal Dialysis Care & Pelvic Organ Prolapse (POP) / 肾脏透析照护、盆底肌松弛与盆腔器官脱垂

Renal Dialysis and Shunt Protection

透析替代治疗与动静脉瘘（分流管）的绝对保护
When chronic renal failure progresses to end-stage renal disease (ESRD), dialysis or a kidney transplant is required to sustain life. Hemodialysis uses an artificial kidney machine to remove toxic wastes and excess water from blood, typically performed three times weekly via an arteriovenous (AV) fistula or graft in the arm. Support workers must never take blood pressure, draw blood, or apply restrictive clothing/bandages to the arm with a vascular access shunt. Peritoneal dialysis uses the client's peritoneal membrane inside the abdomen to filter blood through an indwelling catheter.
当慢性肾功能衰竭进展至尿毒症终末期时，必须依赖血液净化透析或肾移植维持生命。血液透析利用人工肾透析机滤过血液中的代谢毒素与多余水分，通常每周在医院或透析中心进行三次，通过前臂手术吻合的动静脉内瘘（AV瘘）或移植物建立血管通路。支持工作者切记：绝对严禁在造瘘侧手臂测量血压、抽取血样或佩戴紧身衣物手表，以防内瘘受压闭塞；腹膜透析则利用患者自身的腹膜作为半透膜，通过腹腔内置管注入透析液完成交换。

Supporting Clients With Urinary System Disorders

泌尿系统疾病服务对象支持照护原则
Key support worker interventions include: listening with active empathy to alleviate fear and embarrassment; practicing pain-relief comfort measures; maintaining clean, dry, wrinkle-free bedding to protect skin against incontinence moisture; following strict Standard Practices and PPE usage during catheter care; tracking intake and output (I&O) rigorously; and respecting client confidentiality regarding reproductive and urological concerns.
关键照护干预包括：以同理心倾听排解患者面对侵入性检查或失禁的恐惧与尴尬；落实舒缓疼痛的舒适措施；保持床铺清洁、干爽、平整无褶皱，防止大小便失禁浸渍引发失禁性皮炎；在接触导尿管或尿袋时严格执行标准预防和手卫生；极其严密地统计与记录24小时出入液体量（I&O）；严守服务对象关于泌尿生殖健康隐私的职业保密契约。

Reproductive System Disorders: Pelvic Organ Prolapse (POP)

生殖系统疾病：盆底肌功能障碍与盆腔器官脱垂（POP）
Pelvic organ prolapse (POP) occurs when muscles, ligaments, and connective tissues supporting pelvic organs weaken, causing one or more pelvic organs (bladder, uterus, rectum) to drop downward and press into or protrude from the vagina. POP affects many women, particularly older individuals and those who have experienced multiple vaginal births.
盆腔器官脱垂（POP）是指支撑女性盆腔脏器的盆底肌群、筋膜及韧带发生进行性松弛薄弱，导致一个或多个盆腔器官（膀胱、子宫、直肠）下垂移位并向阴道腔膨出甚至脱出阴道口外。POP在绝经后老年女性及有多胎经阴道分娩史的经产妇中发生率极高。
PAGE 808 · 第 808 页 Cystocele, Rectocele, Kegel Exercises & BPH / 膀胱膨出、直肠膨出、凯格尔骨盆底训练与前列腺增生

Types of POP: Cystocele and Rectocele

盆底脱垂类型：膀胱膨出与直肠膨出
A cystocele occurs when the bladder drops downward and pushes against the front wall of the vagina, causing urinary frequency, urgency, incomplete bladder emptying, and recurring UTIs. A rectocele occurs when the rectum bulges forward into the posterior vaginal wall, causing constipation, difficulty with bowel evacuation, and rectal fullness. Risk factors include aging, previous vaginal deliveries, chronic coughing, obesity, heavy lifting, and chronic straining.
膀胱膨出（Cystocele）是指膀胱向后下移位压迫阴道前壁膨出，常引起顽固性尿频、尿急、排尿不尽感及反复发作的膀胱炎。直肠膨出（Rectocele）是指直肠前壁向阴道后壁突出，导致严重便秘、排便费力受阻及会阴部持续下坠胀痛。危险因素涵盖高龄衰老、经阴道产伤、慢性剧烈咳嗽、重度肥胖、经常搬运重物及长期用力排便。

PRACTICE GUIDELINES: Management of Pelvic Organ Prolapse / 执业指南：盆底脱垂的日常护理与凯格尔训练指导

  • Avoid straining during urination or bowel movements. Keep stools soft through adequate dietary fibre and fluids to prevent constipation.
    排尿或排便时切忌用力过度屏气。通过摄入高纤维饮食与充足液体保持大便软通，杜绝便秘加剧脱垂。
  • Instruct client to empty the bladder completely at each void.
    指导服务对象每次排尿时尽可能彻底排空膀胱，减少残余尿量。
  • Monitor and report symptoms of UTI (urgency, frequency, dysuria, burning, cloudy urine).
    密切监测并汇报尿路感染征象（尿急、尿频、尿痛、灼烧感或尿液浑浊）。
  • Support weight loss efforts if obesity is increasing intra-abdominal pressure.
    对于超重服务对象，配合照护方案给予减重支持以减轻腹内压对盆底的持续压迫。
  • Guide and encourage Kegel exercises (pelvic floor muscle contractions) to strengthen the supporting pubococcygeus muscles.
    指导并督促服务对象坚持开展凯格尔运动（Kegel Exercises，骨盆底肌肉收缩训练）以增强耻尾肌支撑力。

Benign Prostatic Hypertrophy (BPH)

良性前列腺增生（BPH）：排尿困难与尿潴留
Benign prostatic hypertrophy (BPH, also called benign prostatic hyperplasia) is a noncancerous enlargement of the prostate gland surrounding the male urethra. As the prostate enlarges, it compresses the urethra, obstructing urine flow from the bladder. Symptoms include urinary hesitancy, a weak or interrupted stream, dribbling at the end of voiding, nocturia (frequent urination at night), and acute urinary retention. Treatment includes medication, minimally invasive laser therapy, or transurethral resection of the prostate (TURP).
良性前列腺增生（BPH）是老年男性前列腺组织的良性非癌性体积增大。由于前列腺环绕于男性后尿道周围，增大的腺体组织压迫狭窄尿道，阻碍膀胱正常排尿。典型症状包括排尿踌躇延缓、尿流变细无力、尿线中断、排尿末滴沥不尽、夜尿频多（Nocturia）以及突发急性尿潴留。临床治疗手段包括药物口服、微创激光消融或经尿道前列腺电切术（TURP）。
PAGE 809 · 第 809 页 Infertility, Reproductive Health & Compassionate Care / 不孕不育心理支持、生殖健康与全人关怀

Infertility and Psychosocial Support

不孕不育综合征与深层心理疏导支持
Infertility is defined as the inability to achieve pregnancy after one full year of regular, unprotected intercourse. Causes can be attributed to male factors (low sperm count or motility), female factors (ovulatory dysfunction, blocked fallopian tubes, endometriosis), or combined and unexplained factors. Facing infertility is deeply distressing, often triggering grief, guilt, depression, relationship strain, and feelings of inadequacy. Support workers must provide active, non-judgmental listening, validate their emotional journey, and respect their dignity.
不孕不育（Infertility）在医学上定义为未采取任何避孕措施、保持规律性生活满一年仍未能受孕。其原因可归结于男方因素（精子数量过少或活力减退）、女方因素（排卵障碍、输卵管堵塞粘连、子宫内膜异位症），或男女双方复合因素及不明原因。不孕往往给当事人及家庭造成深重的情感创伤与悲恸，引发自责、抑郁、伴侣关系紧张及自我价值否定。支持工作者必须以不带偏见的态度用心倾听，肯定其心理创伤，切实呵护其人格尊严。

Comprehensive Compassionate Care in Reproductive Disorders

生殖系统疾病全人关怀核心实践准则
When providing care to clients living with reproductive system disorders: protect the client from infection by following Standard Practices and wearing recommended PPE; practice pain-reducing comfort measures ensuring clean, dry, wrinkle-free bedding and comfortable positioning; listen with genuine empathy when the client expresses anxiety about medical or surgical interventions; and strictly adhere to confidentiality, never discussing the client's private medical diagnosis with anyone outside the immediate care team.
照护患有生殖系统疾病的服务对象时，支持工作者必须恪守以下核心准则：严格执行标准预防原则并穿戴合适PPE以切断感染链；落实疼痛缓解舒适护理，保持床铺整洁干燥平整无褶皱以维持舒适体位；当服务对象对其检查诊断或手术治疗流露焦虑担忧时，给予真挚的同理心倾听；恪尽保密义务，绝对不在照料团队之外与任何人谈论服务对象的私密病情与诊断隐私。
PAGE 810 · 第 810 页 Chapter Review Key Points & Critical Thinking in Practice / 第33章全章复习要点与临床反思实践题

Chapter Review: Key Points Across All Systems

全章核心复习要点：全人多系统病理与PSW照护总揽
  • • Oncology: Cancer includes a group of diseases characterized by out-of-control cell division and growth leading to tumour formation. Metastasis occurs when cancer cells break off and spread to distant organs.
    肿瘤学：癌症是一组以细胞异常失控增殖分裂为特征的疾病群，形成肿瘤肿块；癌细胞脱落经循环扩散至远处器官即发生转移。
  • • Integumentary System: Involves the skin and appendages, including hives (urticaria), dermatitis, eczema, scabies, psoriasis, boils, cysts, impetigo, shingles, and skin cancers.
    皮肤系统：累及皮肤及其附属器，涵盖荨麻疹、皮炎、湿疹、疥疮、银屑病、疖肿、囊肿、脓疱疮、带状疱疹及皮肤癌。
  • • Musculoskeletal System: Conditions affecting mobility and joints include fractures, traction care, cast care, osteomyelitis, amputation, osteoporosis, arthritis, and gout.
    肌肉骨骼系统：影响运动功能与关节的疾病包括骨折、牵引护理、石膏护理、骨髓炎、截肢残端定型、骨质疏松症、关节炎及痛风。
  • • Nervous System: Disruption of neuro-signals affecting physical and cognitive functions: stroke (FAST protocol), epilepsy, ABI/TBI, Parkinson's, Huntington's, MS, ALS, and spinal cord injuries.
    神经系统：中枢与外周神经信号传导中断影响躯体与认知：脑卒中（FAST识别）、癫痫急救、颅脑损伤、帕金森病、亨廷顿病、多发性硬化、渐冻症及脊髓损伤。
  • • Endocrine System: Hormone regulation failures: diabetes mellitus (hypoglycemia vs hyperglycemia, diabetic foot care), hyperthyroidism, and hypothyroidism.
    内分泌系统：激素分泌异常：糖尿病（低血糖与高血糖危象、糖尿病足护理规程）、甲状腺功能亢进与减退症。
  • • Cardiovascular System: Involves heart and blood vessels: hypertension, CAD, angina pectoris, myocardial infarction, CHF, arrhythmias, and phlebitis/DVT.
    心血管系统：累及心脏与血管循环：高血压、冠状动脉疾病、心绞痛、心肌梗死、充血性心力衰竭、心律失常及深静脉血栓形成。
  • • Respiratory System: Asthma, chronic bronchitis, emphysema (COPD), pneumonia, influenza, and tuberculosis (TB).
    呼吸系统：支气管哮喘、慢性支气管炎、肺气肿（慢阻肺）、重症肺炎、流感及结核病。
  • • Immune System: Autoimmune disorders (lupus, MS, RA, scleroderma, Graves, Hashimoto) and immunodeficiency (HIV/AIDS transmission, staging, and anti-stigma care).
    免疫系统：自身免疫病（狼疮、硬皮病、格雷夫斯病、桥本病）与免疫缺陷（HIV/AIDS体液传播、分期及无歧视尊严照护）。
  • • Digestive System: GERD, gallbladder disease/cholecystitis, celiac disease, IBS, Crohn's disease, colitis, and diverticular disease.
    消化系统：胃食管反流病、胆囊炎/胆结石、乳糜泻、肠易激综合征、克罗恩病、结肠炎及结肠憩室病。
  • • Urinary System: UTIs, cystitis, pyelonephritis, renal calculi (urine straining protocol), and acute/chronic renal failure (dialysis and shunt safety).
    泌尿系统：尿路感染、膀胱炎、肾盂肾炎、肾结石滤尿、急慢性肾衰竭（透析血管造瘘保护）。
  • • Reproductive System: Pelvic organ prolapse (cystocele, rectocele, Kegel exercises), benign prostatic hypertrophy (BPH), and infertility psychosocial support.
    生殖系统：盆腔器官脱垂（膀胱/直肠膨出、凯格尔骨盆底训练）、前列腺增生（BPH）及不孕症心理关怀。

CRITICAL THINKING IN PRACTICE: Clinical Case Scenario / 临床反思实践：金·克里斯（Kim Kris）带状疱疹临床案例分析

  • Kim Kris has severe pain on his left side over his kidney area. He does not want to eat and has had difficulty getting out of bed. The doctor has diagnosed him with shingles. Can a person have shingles without having any visible rash? What are your responsibilities when caring for someone who has shingles?
    服务对象金·克里斯（Kim Kris）左侧肾区出现剧烈神经刺痛，食欲不振，甚至难以起床下地。医生最终确诊其患有带状疱疹（Shingles）。思考题：一个人在皮肤表面完全没有出现可见皮疹水疱的情况下，是否可能已经罹患了带状疱疹？在日常照护带状疱疹患者时，支持工作者的核心职业职责包括哪些？
  • Clinical Analysis: Yes, in the early pre-eruptive phase (prodrome), intense neuropathic burning or shooting pain along a dermatome can precede the appearance of classic blistering rash by several days. Furthermore, a rare condition called 'zoster sine herpete' presents with localized neuropathic pain without rash. As a PSW, your responsibilities include: following Contact Precautions until all lesions crust; ensuring non-immune caregivers or pregnant women do not care for the client; applying gentle comfort measures to protect fragile skin; avoiding scratching to prevent secondary bacterial infection; offering loose clothing; monitoring and reporting pain levels for timely analgesic administration; and observing for post-herpetic neuralgia (PHN).
    临床深度解析：是的！在带状疱疹发病前驱期（Prodromal Phase），病毒沿神经节活化可导致受累皮节区产生剧烈的灼烧样或电击样神经痛，常在皮疹水疱出现前数日即已发生；此外临床上极罕见的‘无疹型带状疱疹’（Zoster Sine Herpete）可仅有顽固单侧神经痛而自始至终无肉眼可见皮疹。作为PSW，照护职责包括：在疱疹完全结痂前严格执行接触隔离防护；未患过水痘且未接种疫苗的照护人员及孕妇严禁接触该服务对象；采取轻柔舒适护理保护病损皮肤；防止抓挠以防继发严重细菌感染；协助穿着柔软宽松棉质衣物；密切评估记录疼痛并遵医嘱协助用药；警惕疱疹后神经痛（PHN）并汇报。
Review Questions 1 to 3 are listed below. Questions 4 to 45 continue on physical page 811. Interactive quiz cards with official publisher answer verifications are available in the Review Exam Bank section.
